     3	<!-- SUMMARY -->
     4	
     5	At 2048 events, remember is trace-exact on no run in 7 of 10 models.
     6	Lower median first-error step on inexact main remember runs at 2048: 3 (4 exact runs excluded, 80 runs).
     7	Pin ticks bitwise equal to the certified row: 163840 of 163840.
     8	Pin trace-exact runs at 2048: 80 of 80.
     9	Window has no trace-exact run at 1024 in 10 of 10 models.
    10	OLMo checkpoints: olmo2-7b-5B at 1024: pin 0/4, remember 0/4; olmo2-7b-84B at 1024: pin 4/4, remember 1/4; olmo2-7b-base at 1024: pin 4/4, remember 0/4.
    11	The three numbers above are the json count. rem_recount.py reads the csv and agrees.
    12	
    13	<!-- /SUMMARY -->
    14	
    15	## Question
    16	
    17	An LLM's exact computation is certifiable only if it forgets, and this holds in every model. UNIVERSAL showed that each of 10 base models has a row finder, that a rank-1 edit makes each an exact pinned executor, and that 59 long pinned runs are exact and bitwise. This experiment keeps that tick, those certified tables, and that edited model, and changes only the cache policy so the history stays in context.
    18	
    19	## Protocol
    20	
    21	The counted edit is the checkpoint whose G1 file certified the tables. No new training. LoRA is loaded with `PeftModel.from_pretrained` and left unmerged: a bf16 merge can drop a rank-1 delta, and UNIVERSAL's G1 ran unmerged. Each job loads the base and the adapter from scratch. Qwen3-4B's vector is BIGIDEA's, at `/bi/scr-6b/E7-steer-L18-block-s100/step100`. The other paths are on `polyformer-universal-results`, mounted at `/uni`.
    22	
    23	- E7 step 100, one vector at layer L//2, tick positions only: the five Qwen3 sizes and Phi-4-mini. Phi's counted file is step 100. The 300-step continuation certified fewer tables and lost the only table above 16 rows.
    24	- E3 step 100, rank-1 LoRA, unmerged: Mistral-7B, OLMo-2-7B-Instruct, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B.
    25	- OLMo emergence arm: E7 step 100 on the three pretraining checkpoints UNIVERSAL used (5B, 84B, released base). The main-panel OLMo row is the instruct model with E3. The emergence row is E7. That split is the one UNIVERSAL trained.
    26	
    27	Tables. Seed 777, the same 128-table draw as UNIVERSAL. For a main model, among tables its counted G1 file marks `pass`, take the lowest index in each nonempty size bin (4–16, 17–64, 65–128, 129–256), then the lowest remaining certified indices, until four. The chosen rows are `TABLES` in `rem_models.py`. `python rem_models.py` checks them against the local G1 json. The job draws the tables again and aborts if the shape, the row count, or the state and event counts disagree with that row.
    28	
    29	Re-certify on the path that is run (`pb_pin.certify`, batch-1, full-vocabulary argmax, margin > 0), in the same process as the tapes. A main table that fails re-certification aborts the job: that is the wrong weights, not a result. The same abort applies to an OLMo table whose G1 file passed. OLMo-5B's two tables did not pass. The job records the failure and still runs pin and remember. Trace-exactness is against the true machine. Bitwise equality is against the certified logits of a row, and a stable wrong row can still match.
    30	
    31	OLMo uses two indices, the two lowest certified by both the 84B checkpoint and the released base: 29 and 45. The same indices are used at 5B. 5B certified neither.
    32	
    33	Tapes. Seed 20261028. Four tapes of `T = max(lengths)` event indices, `Random(20261028 + table_i * 100003)` then `randrange(n_events)`. Cells at 256 and 1024 are prefixes of the 4096-event tape, not new draws. The same index sequence is the same tape on every tokenizer. The same tapes are used for every policy. `exact_at` is true at length L when the first mismatch is at index L or later, or there is no mismatch. The first-error step is 1-based.
    34	
    35	Policies. The bodies are `bi_stream.Run`. Nothing else about the tick changes.
    36	
    37	- **pin** is `PIN`: `pb_pin.tick`. Π is kept. The block `[s][e]` is computed on a clone at positions `|Π|` and `|Π|+1`, and the clone is discarded. This is the certified code path, so a bitwise comparison is against the certificate. Pin runs the whole tape, including after a drift, so every tick can be checked. The fed pair is compared when it is a row of the table.
    38	- **remember** is `FULL`: token by token, nothing evicted, text positions continuing. The model emits the state and that argmax is fed back.
    39	- **window** is `SW4b`. k = 4, the middle of the widths H2b ran (2, 4, 8), with StreamingLLM's cache-relative re-rotation (`endswith("b")`). Each token sees Π plus the previous 3 stream tokens. Π's keys are re-rotated to sit just in front of that window. Π is the sink. It is not dropped. The parenthetical in the brief, that the window fails because it loses Π, describes a different policy. This one keeps Π. A failure with Π still in the cache is the registered mechanism: the cached window token was written while it could see the previous event.
    40	
    41	Remember and window stop at the first wrong state, before that state is fed. Exactness and the first-error step are already determined. Pin does not stop early.
    42	
    43	The argmax is `pb_pin.top2`, the same tie break as the certificate. The trace is compared to the true machine, computed from the tape before the run, not to δ(model state, event) after the state has drifted.
    44	
    45	Error kinds, with the event streamed in so the model is not asked to emit it. At the first wrong tick the fed state is still the true state. **Step:** the argmax is a state of the table and is not δ(s, e). **Reading:** the argmax is not a state (an event, the newline, or another token). The record also stores which of those the argmax was, whether it equals δ(s′, e) for some other state s′, and whether it copies the fed state.
    46	
    47	Two adaptations of `bi_stream`, both recorded in the job file. `rotate_kv` on Qwen3 is `bi_stream.rotate_kv` unchanged, and the job aborts unless the two agree bitwise. On other models the same increment uses that model's `rotate_half`, and only the rotary subspace when the head is only partly rotated. If `attention_scaling` is not 1, the increment uses the unscaled angles so the scale is not applied twice. Before any tape, `R(d) R(p)` is compared to the model's own `apply_rotary_pos_emb` at `p+d`. On a sliding-window cache layer, the policy's selected keys are cropped to the last `sliding_window − 1` and `cumulative_length` is set to that count, which is the cache that layer keeps. Full-attention layers receive every selected key. Gemma's sliding window, and any hybrid `layer_types`, stay as the model defines them. The job records `n_sliding_layers`, `sliding_window`, and the layer-type counts.
    48	
    49	Bitwise equality is `torch.equal` on the bf16 logits, inside the job, on the same host and the same code path as the certificate. TF32 settings are left at the library default. Attention is the implementation in `uni_models.MODELS` (Gemma eager, the others sdpa).
    50	
    51	Models load from the read-only snapshot on `polyformer-universal-hf` (mounted at `/hf`). The job resolves `refs/<revision>` and reads the snapshot directory. `HF_HOME` is `/tmp/hf`, so the mount is not written. `polyformer-universal-results` is mounted read-only at `/uni`. `polyformer-bigidea-results` is mounted read-only at `/bi`. Writes go to `polyformer-remember-results`.
    52	
    53	GPU. A10G for Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-1.7B, Phi-4-mini, and Qwen3-4B, which is where UNIVERSAL ran them. The function requests Modal's `A10` name at the same list price, and the ledger field stays `A10G`. L40S for the other six main models and the three OLMo checkpoints: the cheapest GPU that holds the bf16 weights. Not H100.
    54	
    55	OLMo counted jobs are pin and remember only, at 1024, on tables 29 and 45, four tapes. The main panel is pin, remember, and window at 256, 1024, and 4096, four tapes, four tables.
    56	
    57	Every result number in the results section is written by `rem_report.py`. The three numbers it checks against `rem_recount.py` are: how many of the main models have zero trace-exact remember runs at the plan's key length (4096 unless a cut amends it); the lower median, at index `(n−1)//2` of the sorted values, of the 1-based first-error step over those remember runs, with runs that are exact at that length left out; and the pin ticks bitwise-equal to the certified row over the pin ticks, counting the full tape once (the csv puts those fields only on the row whose length is T).
    58	
    59	## Registered predictions
    60	
    61	These paragraphs stay as written. A smoke amendment is inserted by `rem_smoke_read.py` before any counted job, under Amendments, and only by the rules in that file.
    62	
    63	**Pin.** Probability 0.90. Every main (model, table, tape) pin run is trace-exact at 256, 1024, and 4096. Each of those tables re-certifies. Every pin tick is bitwise equal to its certified row. A smoke in which pin is not exact, or not bitwise, is a bug in the load or the tick. It is not a reason to predict that pin fails.
    64	
    65	**Remember.** Probability 0.80 that each of the 10 models has zero trace-exact remember runs at 4096. Probability 0.95 that the lower median first-error step on the inexact main remember runs at 4096 is below 4096. Point median 32. 90% range 2 to 512. Savant's full-attention arm in H2 Part A failed by steps 2 to 8. The range is wider because the other families and the larger tables may drift later.
    66	
    67	**Window.** Probability 0.75 that each model has zero trace-exact window runs at 256, and so on the longer prefixes of those same tapes. Point median 8. 90% range 2 to 128. Probability 0.70 that this happens while Π is still the sink. `SW4b` keeps Π. The failure mode registered here is the one H2 measured: the token kept in the window was written while it could see the previous event.
    68	
    69	**OLMo pin.** Probability 0.60. At 1024, trace-exact pin runs are 0 of 8 at 5B, 8 of 8 at 84B, and 8 of 8 at the released base. Eight is two tables by four tapes. 5B failing certification is the emergence result. Bitwise equality is a separate record: a wrong row can be stable.
    70	
    71	**OLMo remember.** Probability 0.80. Zero of 8 trace-exact remember runs at 1024, at each of the three checkpoints.
    72	
    73	## Cost and cuts
    74	
    75	The cap is $20, in `rem_modal.CAP_USD`, recorded in the ledger before any spend. Rates are the Modal list prices in `rem_modal.py` (GPU plus 8 CPU at 0.0000131 per second plus 48 GiB at 0.00000222 per second). A reservation is that rate times max_seconds + 30 + 240. Reserved amounts count against the cap until the job settles. Settlement is the same rate times the wall time, capped at max_seconds + 30, plus the 240-second startup. The host CPU is unknown until the job runs; the ledger records the GPU at reserve and the GPU and CPU at settle.

   111	<!-- /COUNTED-COST -->
   112	
   113	`rem_cost.py` applies this ladder until the sum of counted reservations fits in the cap minus what the ledger has already reserved or settled. It never drops a model.
   114	
   115	1. Tapes 4 to 2.
   116	2. Drop recorded length 256. Those cells are prefixes of the long tape, so the compute does not change. The ladder records the cut and continues.
   117	3. Long length 4096 to 2048. Measured claims are then read at 2048. The prediction paragraphs above stay as written.
   118	4. Tapes 2 to 1.
   119	5. Long length 2048 to 1024.
   120	
   121	If the last rung still does not fit, stop and do not launch. Spawn only as many jobs as the remaining cap can reserve at once. Settle a wave before reserving the next. A timeout is not a trace failure. An incomplete job is not a counted result.
   122	
   123	## What could be wrong
   124	
   125	The verdicts are in the results section, filled from the job files. The checks are:
   126	
   127	1. **Pin is not the certified function** (wrong tick, a merged LoRA, a stale adapter). Ruled out when every main table re-certifies, every pin tick is bitwise equal to its certified row, and the edit file's sha256 is in the job record.
   128	2. **Remember evicts tokens or restarts positions.** Ruled out when every remember run has `stream_tokens = 2 × ticks` and `max_delta = 0`.
   129	3. **The result is one architecture's sliding window, or tables that were never certified.** Ruled out when a model with `n_sliding_layers = 0` is pin-exact and has no trace-exact remember run on the same tapes, and the main tables re-certified. Phi's certified tables are small. The other models carry the size spread.

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba REMEMBER2.md' in /Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/remember/research/remember
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# REMEMBER-2
     2	
     3	<!-- SUMMARY -->
     4	
     5	E-hist under remember, exact at 256: 4 of 16.
     6	Lower median first-error step on inexact E-hist remember runs at 4096: 26 (0 exact runs excluded, 16 runs).
     7	E-hist under pin, exact at 4096: 7 of 16.
     8	The three numbers above are the json count. r2_recount.py reads the csv and agrees.
     9	E-pin under pin, exact at 4096: 16 of 16. E-pin under remember, lower median first error at 4096: 3.
    10	
    11	<!-- /SUMMARY -->
    12	
    13	## Question
    14	
    15	REMEMBER found pin trace-exact on 80 of 80 runs at 2048 events, and the same weights with the history kept failed at a median first error of step 3. A referee can say that those edits were learned in pinned format, so the failure is a format shift. This run trains the same edit class on the remember presentation and measures both edits under both policies as the tape grows.
    16	
    17	## Protocol
    18	
    19	Four models: Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B. Two edits each.
    20	
    21	E-pin is the checkpoint REMEMBER measured. It is not retrained. Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B were measured as an E7 vector, and UNIVERSAL did not train an E3 for them. Reusing the edit means not training a new pinned LoRA. E-hist is a new edit of that same class: E7 for both Qwen models, rank-1 LoRA for Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B. A Qwen LoRA beside an E7 pin would mix the format with the parameterization. Paths and the E-pin sha256 values are `pin_path` and `PIN_SHA` in `r2_models.py`. The eval aborts if the E-pin file does not match.
    22	
    23	E-hist uses UNIVERSAL's optimizer and schedule. 100 steps, warmup 5, AdamW betas (0.9, 0.99), weight decay 0, gradient clip 1.0, seed 1. The data RNG is `Random(1 + 7919)`. Learning rate 2e-2 for E7 and 2e-3 for E3. E7 may retry once at a quarter of the learning rate when the run diverged and the elapsed time times 2.5 is still under max_seconds. Diverged means a non-finite loss, or the mean accuracy of the last 10 steps more than 0.02 below the mean of steps 21 through 30. The first checkpoint is written to `edit/chosen` before the retry, so a kill leaves it. The retry replaces it only when the retry's pinned validation accepts more tables, then more rows. E3 does not retry. The saved edit is step 100 unless that retry wins.
    24	
    25	Validation is 128 tables from `Random(101)` on the held-out name pool, scored with UNIVERSAL's packed fast eval at steps 10, 30, and 100, plus 4 teacher-forced history trajectories. The history score is recorded and is not used to choose the checkpoint. The validation tables are not the REMEMBER tables.
    26	
    27	A UNIVERSAL step supervises 48 × 16 = 768 event positions. E-hist keeps 768 by training on 3 trajectories of 256 events, not on 48 independent pinned blocks. The number of tables in a step drops from 48 to 3, because each trajectory contributes 256 losses. The length is 256, not a mixture. "Up to 256" is that cap. A miss at 256 is then either cross-table generalization, when the training accuracy is high, or a failure to learn the history, when it is low. Both answer Q1.
    28	
    29	The loss is cross-entropy on the true destination, teacher-forced along the true trace. The sequence is Π, including the tokenizer's document prefix, then s0 e0 s1 e1 … with s_{k+1} = δ(s_k, e_k). Positions are 0, 1, 2, … through the whole sequence. They are not reset to |Π| and |Π|+1. Attention is causal: each token sees Π and the run so far, not a block-diagonal mask. Loss is only on the event token, the second token of each tick. The steer mask is 0 on Π and on padding and 1 on the stream tokens. `use_cache` is false. Gradient checkpointing uses `use_reentrant` false, and `enable_input_require_grads` runs before the base is frozen, the same order as UNIVERSAL. The E7 vector is added only on stream tokens and is off during Π's prefill.
    30	
    31	Policies are the ones REMEMBER ran. Pin is `bi_stream` PIN: Π is kept, the block is computed on a clone at positions |Π| and |Π|+1, and the clone is discarded. Remember is FULL: nothing is evicted, positions continue, and the run stops at the first wrong state, before that state is fed. There is no window. The patches are `rem_job`'s: `rotate_kv`, the sliding-window crop, and the rope check before any tape.
    32	
    33	Tables are REMEMBER's four certified tables per model, redrawn at seed 777. Tapes use seed 20261028, the same generator as REMEMBER. Four tapes. Lengths 256, 1024, 4096, and 8192. A shorter length is a prefix of the long tape. The smoke length 64 is a prefix of that same generator. Trace-exactness is against the true machine. `exact_at` is true at L when the first mismatch is at index L or later, or there is none. `first_error` is the 0-based index. The first-error step is 1-based. The lower median is the value at index (n−1)//2 of the sorted values.
    34	
    35	E-pin is re-certified in the same process. A failed table aborts the job. E-hist failing certification is a result for Q3, and the tapes still run. Bitwise equality is `torch.equal` on the bf16 logits against the certificate computed in that process. Pin runs the whole tape. The margin at each recorded length is kept for every run that executed that step, and the full trace is stored decimated to at most 96 points, always including step 1, the last step, and the first-error step.
    36	
    37	LoRA stays unmerged. Each job loads the base and one edit from scratch. Bases come from the read-only snapshot on `polyformer-universal-hf` (`/hf`). E-pin files come from `polyformer-universal-results` (`/uni`) and `polyformer-bigidea-results` (`/bi`). Writes go only to `polyformer-remember2-results` (`/vol`). The hub is offline. Models are not downloaded locally. Results go to `research/remember/results2`, so REMEMBER's `results/plan.json` is left alone.
    38	
    39	Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B use the A10G price class. The Modal function requests `gpu="A10"`, and the ledger field stays `A10G`. Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B use L40S. An out-of-memory failure before a complete result is replaced on the next GPU: L40S for an A10G model, H100 for an L40S model, under a new job id. The amendment records it. Measured claims come from the complete replacement.
    40	
    41	A timeout is not a trace failure. The retry has a new job id and `--carry` of the finished cells. The complete `runs.json` is the file the plan's job id names. An incomplete job is not a counted result.
    42	
    43	The three numbers `r2_report.py` checks against `r2_recount.py` are: how many E-hist remember runs are exact at the training length, 256; the lower median first-error step of the E-hist remember runs that are inexact at the plan's key length, together with how many exact runs were excluded and how many runs there were; and how many E-hist pin runs are exact at that key length. The report reads `runs.json`. The recount reads `runs.csv` and does not import the report. The training length and the key length come from `results2/plan.json`. A train sha256 that does not match the E-hist file the eval loaded is an exit, and that edit is not published.
    44	
    45	## Registered predictions
    46	
    47	These paragraphs stay as written. A smoke amendment is inserted by `r2_smoke_read.py` before any counted job, under Amendments, and only by the rules in that file.
    48	
    49	**E-pin, pin.** Probability 0.95. Every E-pin run under pin is trace-exact at 256, 1024, 4096, and 8192, and every pin tick is bitwise equal to the certificate made in that process. A smoke in which E-pin under pin is not exact, or not bitwise, is a bug. It is not a reason to change this sentence.
    50	
    51	**E-pin, remember.** Probability 0.85 that the lower median first-error step on the inexact E-pin remember runs at 8192 is at most 8. Point median 3. 90% range 2 to 32. Probability 0.80 that fewer than half of the E-pin remember runs are exact at 256.
    52	
    53	**E-hist, remember, at the training length.** Probability 0.65 that, in at least 3 of the 4 models, at least half of that model's E-hist remember runs are exact at 256. Point fraction 0.75. 90% range 0.25 to 1.
    54	
    55	**E-hist, remember, as the tape grows.** Probability 0.70 that the fraction exact at 8192 is strictly below the fraction exact at 256. Probability 0.75 that the lower median first-error step on the inexact E-hist remember runs at 8192 is above 32. Point median 512. 90% range 16 to 4096. The shape this names is drift past the training length, the pattern BRIDGE saw, rather than a failure at step 3.
    56	
    57	**E-hist, pin.** Probability 0.60 that every E-hist pin run is trace-exact at every recorded length. The doubt is that the edit never saw positions reset to |Π| and |Π|+1.
    58	
    59	The sentences name 8192 events and 4 tapes. If the cost ladder shortens the tape or the tape count, the measured lines say which length and which n were run. These sentences are not edited.
    60	
    61	## Cost and cuts
    62	
    63	The cap is $30, in `r2_modal.CAP_USD`, recorded in the ledger before any spend. Rates are the Modal list prices in `r2_modal.py`: H100 0.001097, L40S 0.000542, A10G 0.000306, plus 8 CPU at 0.0000131 per second and 48 GiB at 0.00000222 per second. A reservation is that rate times max_seconds + 30 + 240. Reserved amounts count against the cap until the job settles. Settlement is the same rate times the wall, capped at max_seconds + 30, plus the 240-second startup. The ledger records the GPU at reserve and the GPU and the host CPU at settle.

    91	
    92	The smoke is one job, `smoke-qwen3-1.7b`, on Qwen3-1.7B, table 8, A10G, max_seconds 1500. It trains 2 steps at the counted batch shape, with no validation and no retry, then evaluates both edits under both policies at length 64 on one tape. It also times 256 pin ticks and 256 remember ticks on E-pin, with remember marks every 32 ticks and no early stop. The 2-step weights are not the counted edit. E-hist's smoke exactness does not amend a prediction.
    93	
    94	Smoke rules, applied by `r2_smoke_read.py` and not by hand. If any E-pin remember run is exact at the smoke length L, the amendment sets the point median to 2L and the 90% range to 2 through 3L. If an E-pin remember first-error step falls outside 2 to 32, the amendment widens the range to include the observed steps. E-pin under pin not exact, not bitwise, a failed E-pin re-certification, or any remember run with `stream_tokens` other than 2 × ticks or `max_delta` other than 0, is a bug: the reader exits 2 and writes nothing.
    95	
    96	`r2_cost.py` reads `timing.json` and walks this ladder until the train reservations and the eval reservations both fit in the cap minus what the ledger has already reserved or settled. It does not drop a model or an edit. It does not drop 256: those cells are prefixes, so dropping them saves no compute.

   103	6. Long length 2048 to 1024.
   104	
   105	The projection, fixed here. Pin seconds per tick is the probe's pin seconds divided by its pin ticks, scaled by (S / 1.7)^0.5 for a model of nominal size S. Remember seconds to reach T ticks are a least-squares fit of a T + b T² to the probe marks. A negative coefficient is set to zero and the other coefficient is refit. Remember seconds scale by S / 1.7 and are at least the pin price of the same ticks. A training step scales by S / 1.7. The first smoke step is the compile step and the last is the steady step. Counted training is the compile step plus 99 steady steps, plus the load, plus a validation budget at each of the three checkpoints: 128 packed tables at a quarter of one trajectory forward, and 4 history trajectories. An eval load scales by (S / 1.7)^0.5, and an eval pays for two loads. E-hist remember is priced as a full tape. E-pin remember is priced as pin ticks up to min(T, max(64, 8 times the greatest smoke first-error step)) when every smoke E-pin remember run failed, and as a full remember tape when any of those runs was exact or that tick budget reaches T. The eval also prices two certifications and two full pin tapes. max_seconds is ceil(1.35 times the projection plus 180), and at least 120. A projection that needs more than 24000 seconds does not fit in one container, because the function timeout is 30000 and the subprocess must stop first.
   106	
   107	If the last rung still does not fit, `r2_cost.py` exits 2, writes that fact, and launches nothing.
   108	
   109	Train jobs are spawned and settled before any eval job. The eval's `--hist-path` is `/vol/train-{model}/edit/chosen`. The weights have to be on the volume before the eval starts.
   110	
   111	## What could be wrong
   112	
   113	The verdicts are in the results section, filled from the job files. The checks are:
   114	
   115	1. **E-pin is not the certified function.** Ruled out when every E-pin table re-certifies, every E-pin pin run is trace-exact at the tape length, the bitwise-equal pin ticks equal the pin ticks, none are off-table, there is no logit mismatch, and each E-pin sha256 matches `PIN_SHA`.
   116	2. **Remember evicts or restarts positions.** Ruled out when every remember run, both edits, has `stream_tokens = 2 × ticks` and `max_delta = 0`.
   117	3. **E-hist was trained as pinned blocks.** Ruled out when each train record has 100 steps, trajectory length 256, 768 supervised positions, `causal_mismatches` 0, `presentation.ok`, and the saved example passes `presentation_ok` again at report time, and the train sha256 equals the sha256 of the E-hist file the eval loaded.
   118	
   119	## Amendments

   194	
   195	## First-error anatomy
   196	
   197	One count per run. Step: the argmax is a state of the table and not the true next state. Reading: the argmax is not a state. Remember stops at that state. Pin runs the whole tape.
   198	
   199	e-pin pin: 16 runs, 16 exact through the tape, lower median first error undefined. Kind: none. Argmax: none. Copy of the fed state: 0. Other row of the same event: 0.
   200	e-pin remember: 16 runs, 0 exact through the tape, lower median first error 3. Kind: reading 8, step 8. Argmax: event 2, newline 4, other 2, state 8. Copy of the fed state: 1. Other row of the same event: 7.
   201	e-hist pin: 16 runs, 7 exact through the tape, lower median first error 22. Kind: reading 3, step 6. Argmax: newline 1, other 2, state 6. Copy of the fed state: 5. Other row of the same event: 3.
   202	e-hist remember: 16 runs, 0 exact through the tape, lower median first error 26. Kind: reading 8, step 8. Argmax: newline 2, other 6, state 8. Copy of the fed state: 1. Other row of the same event: 7.
   203	
   204	E-hist remember, lower median first error among runs that are inexact at that length:
   205	qwen3-1.7b: 256 2, 1024 2, 4096 2.
   206	qwen3-4b: 256 5, 1024 5, 4096 5.
   207	llama3.1-8b: 256 26, 1024 67, 4096 67.
   208	mistral-7b: 256 29, 1024 29, 4096 29.
   209

    34	def log(**kw):
    35	    import json
    36	    print(json.dumps(kw, default=str), flush=True)

    38	
    39	def fast_eval(model, pool, prefix, tables, steer):
    40	    """UNIVERSAL's pinned fast_eval, one packed table at a time."""
    41	    import torch
    42	    io, nl = pool["id_of"], pool["nl"]
    43	    c = U.core(model)
    44	    device = c.lm_head.weight.device
    45	    out = []
    46	    was = model.training
    47	    model.eval()
    48	    for tb in tables:
    49	        pi = list(prefix) + pb_pin.encode_table(tb, io, nl)
    50	        ex = {"pi": pi, "blocks": [(io[s], io[e]) for s, e, _t in tb["rows"]],
    51	              "labels": [io[t] for _s, _e, t in tb["rows"]]}
    52	        ids, pos, allow, r, cidx, lab = pb_pin.packed_batch([ex], 0)
    53	        if steer is not None and steer.where == "block":
    54	            steer.mask = (torch.arange(ids.shape[1], device=device) >= len(pi))[None]
    55	        with torch.no_grad():
    56	            h = c.model(input_ids=ids.to(device), position_ids=pos.to(device),
    57	                        attention_mask=U.model_mask(c, allow.to(device)),
    58	                        use_cache=False).last_hidden_state
    59	            lg = U.head_logits(c, h[r.to(device), cidx.to(device)])
    60	        if steer is not None:
    61	            steer.mask = None
    62	        v, i = torch.topk(lg, 2, dim=-1)
    63	        lab = lab.to(lg.device)
    64	        ok = (i[:, 0] == lab) & (v[:, 0] - v[:, 1] > 0)
    65	        out.append({"rows": len(ex["labels"]), "rows_wrong": int((~ok).sum()), "pass": bool(ok.all())})
    66	        del h, lg
    67	    if was:
    68	        model.train()
    69	    rows = sum(item["rows"] for item in out)
    70	    wrong = sum(item["rows_wrong"] for item in out)
    71	    return {"tables": len(out), "accepted": sum(item["pass"] for item in out), "rows": rows,
    72	            "rows_right": rows - wrong, "row_acc": (rows - wrong) / max(1, rows)}
    73	
    74	
    75	def history_acc(model, pool, prefix, tables, steer, length, rng):
    76	    """Teacher-forced accuracy on remember-format trajectories. Not used to choose a checkpoint."""
    77	    import torch
    78	    c = U.core(model)
    79	    device = c.lm_head.weight.device
    80	    was = model.training
    81	    model.eval()
    82	    seen = correct = 0
    83	    with torch.no_grad():
    84	        for tb in tables:
    85	            ex = r2_data.rollout(rng, tb, pool, length)
    86	            ex["pi"] = list(prefix) + ex["pi"]
    87	            ids, pos, allow, r, cidx, lab, steer_mask, _stats = r2_data.history_tensors([ex])
    88	            if steer is not None:
    89	                steer.mask = steer_mask.to(device)
    90	            h = c.model(input_ids=ids.to(device), position_ids=pos.to(device),
    91	                        attention_mask=U.model_mask(c, allow.to(device)), use_cache=False).last_hidden_state
    92	            lg = U.head_logits(c, h[r.to(device), cidx.to(device)])
    93	            if steer is not None:
    94	                steer.mask = None
    95	            correct += int((lg.argmax(-1) == lab.to(device)).sum())
    96	            seen += int(lab.shape[0])
    97	            del h, lg
    98	    if was:
    99	        model.train()
   100	    return {"trajectories": len(tables), "length": length, "tokens": seen,
   101	            "acc": correct / max(1, seen)}
   102	
   103	
   104	def _causal_mismatches(allow, n):
   105	    import torch
   106	    idx = torch.arange(n, device=allow.device)
   107	    want = idx[:, None] >= idx[None, :]
   108	    return int((allow[0, 0, :n, :n] != want).sum())
   109	
   110	
   111	def _run_once(model, pool, prefix, name, out_dir, steps, traj_len, n_traj, val_tables, lr, kind, steer_layer):
   112	    import torch
   113	    c = U.core(model)
   114	    torch.manual_seed(M.TRAIN_SEED)
   115	    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
   116	    model.enable_input_require_grads()
   117	    model.config.use_cache = False
   118	    steer = None
   119	    if kind == "e7":
   120	        for p in model.parameters():
   121	            p.requires_grad_(False)
   122	        steer = U.Steer(model, steer_layer, "block", None)
   123	        params = [steer.vec]
   124	        edit = {"kind": "steer", "layer": steer_layer, "where": "block"}
   125	    else:
   126	        from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model
   127	        names = {n.split(".")[-1] for n, _mod in c.named_modules()}
   128	        targets = ["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"] if "q_proj" in names else ["qkv_proj", "o_proj"]
   129	        cfg = LoraConfig(r=1, lora_alpha=2, lora_dropout=0.0, bias="none", task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
   130	                         target_modules=targets, layers_to_transform=None)
   131	        model = get_peft_model(model, cfg)
   132	        c = U.core(model)
   133	        params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
   134	        edit = {"kind": "lora", "rank": 1, "targets": targets, "layers": None}
   135	    model.config.use_cache = False
   136	    U.core(model).config.use_cache = False
   137	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=lr, betas=(0.9, 0.99), weight_decay=0.0)
   138	    rng = random.Random(M.TRAIN_SEED + 7919)
   139	    supervised = n_traj * traj_len
   140	    rec = {"kind": kind, "edit": edit, "lr": lr, "steps": steps, "warmup": M.WARMUP,
   141	           "trainable_params": int(sum(p.numel() for p in params)), "log": [], "val": {},
   142	           "presentation": {"ok": False, "steps_checked": 0, "causal_mismatches": None, "first_batch": None,
   143	                            "supervised_per_step": None}}
   144	    t0 = time.time()
   145	    log(train=kind, model=name, lr=lr, trainable_params=rec["trainable_params"], supervised=supervised,
   146	        traj_len=traj_len, n_traj=n_traj)
   147	    for step in range(steps):
   148	        cur = lr * min(1.0, (step + 1) / max(1, M.WARMUP))
   149	        for g in opt.param_groups:
   150	            g["lr"] = cur
   151	        model.train()
   152	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
   153	        exs = []
   154	        for _ in range(n_traj):
   155	            ex = r2_data.history_example(rng, pool, traj_len, "train")
   156	            ex["pi"] = list(prefix) + ex["pi"]
   157	            exs.append(ex)
   158	        loss_sum, ok_n, labels_n = 0.0, 0, 0
   159	        log(step_begin=step + 1)
   160	        for ex in exs:
   161	            ids, pos, allow, r, cidx, lab, steer_mask, stats = r2_data.history_tensors([ex])
   162	            for stat in stats:
   163	                if not r2_data.presentation_ok(stat, traj_len):
   164	                    raise RuntimeError(f"history presentation failed: {stat}")
   165	            if step == 0 and rec["presentation"]["first_batch"] is None:
   166	                rec["presentation"]["causal_mismatches"] = _causal_mismatches(allow, stats[0]["seq_len"])
   167	                if rec["presentation"]["causal_mismatches"] != 0:
   168	                    raise RuntimeError("history mask is not causal")
   169	                rec["presentation"]["first_batch"] = stats[0]
   170	            device = c.lm_head.weight.device
   171	            if steer is not None:
   172	                steer.mask = steer_mask.to(device)
   173	                if int(steer.mask.sum()) != stats[0]["steer_on_stream"]:
   174	                    raise RuntimeError("steer mask does not match the stream")
   175	            h = c.model(input_ids=ids.to(device), position_ids=pos.to(device),
   176	                        attention_mask=U.model_mask(c, allow.to(device)), use_cache=False).last_hidden_state
   177	            logits = U.head_logits(c, h[r.to(device), cidx.to(device)])
   178	            tgt = lab.to(device)
   179	            loss = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(logits, tgt, reduction="sum")
   180	            (loss / supervised).backward()
   181	            if steer is not None:
   182	                steer.mask = None
   183	            loss_sum += float(loss.detach())
   184	            ok_n += int((logits.argmax(-1) == tgt).sum())
   185	            labels_n += int(tgt.shape[0])
   186	            del h, loss, logits
   187	        if labels_n != supervised:
   188	            raise RuntimeError(f"step supervised {labels_n} != {supervised}")
   189	        if step == 0:
   190	            rec["presentation"]["supervised_per_step"] = labels_n
   191	        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
   192	        opt.step()
   193	        rec["presentation"]["steps_checked"] += 1
   194	        entry = {"step": step + 1, "loss": round(loss_sum / supervised, 5), "acc": round(ok_n / supervised, 5),
   195	                 "lr": cur, "grad_norm": round(float(gn), 4), "elapsed": round(time.time() - t0, 1)}
   196	        rec["log"].append(entry)
   197	        log(**entry)
   198	        if (step + 1) in M.CKPTS or step + 1 == steps:
   199	            model.eval()
   200	            dest = out_dir / "edit" / f"step{step + 1}"
   201	            _save(model, steer, kind, dest)
   202	            if val_tables:
   203	                rec["val"][str(step + 1)] = {
   204	                    "pin": fast_eval(model, pool, prefix, val_tables, steer),
   205	                    "remember": history_acc(model, pool, prefix, val_tables[:M.HIST_VAL_N], steer, traj_len,
   206	                                            random.Random(M.VAL_SEED + 17 + step)),
   207	                }
   208	                log(kind=kind, step=step + 1, val=rec["val"][str(step + 1)])
   209	            _save(model, steer, kind, out_dir / "edit" / "chosen")
   210	    rec["presentation"]["ok"] = rec["presentation"]["steps_checked"] == steps
   211	    accs = [e["acc"] for e in rec["log"]]
   212	    losses = [e["loss"] for e in rec["log"]]
   213	    tail = sum(accs[-10:]) / len(accs[-10:])
   214	    mid = sum(accs[20:30]) / len(accs[20:30]) if len(accs) >= 30 else None
   215	    rec["diverged"] = bool(any(not math.isfinite(x) for x in losses) or (mid is not None and tail < mid - 0.02))
   216	    rec["acc_steps_21_30"], rec["acc_last10"] = mid, tail
   217	    rec["wall"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   218	    _disable(model, c, kind)
   219	    model.eval()
   220	    return model, steer, rec
   221	
   222	
   223	def _disable(model, c, kind):
   224	    base = c if kind == "e3" else model
   225	    base.gradient_checkpointing_disable()
   226	    base.disable_input_require_grads()
   227	
   228	
   229	def _save(model, steer, kind, dest):
   230	    dest = pathlib.Path(dest)
   231	    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   232	    if kind == "e7":
   233	        steer.save(dest)
   234	    else:
   235	        model.save_pretrained(dest)
   236	
   237	
   238	def _finish_record(rec, kind, dest, host, name):
   239	    weight = r2_load.weight_file(dest, kind)
   240	    rec["sha256"] = r2_load.file_sha256(weight)
   241	    rec["weight"] = weight.name
   242	    rec["chosen"] = str(dest)
   243	    rec["model"] = name
   244	    rec["host"] = host
   245	    rec["at"] = stamp()
   246	    rec["complete"] = True
   247	    return rec
   248	
   249	
   250	def train_edit(name, out_dir, steps, traj_len, n_traj, val_n, max_seconds, allow_retry):
   251	    """Train one E-hist edit from a freshly loaded base. Writes train.json and edit/chosen."""
   252	    import gc
   253	    import torch
   254	    out_dir = pathlib.Path(out_dir)
   255	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   256	    kind = M.kind(name)
   257	    lr = M.LR[kind]
   258	    host = host_record()
   259	    t0 = time.time()
   260	    tok = r2_load.tokenizer(name)
   261	    pool = U.name_pool(tok)
   262	    prefix = U.doc_prefix(tok)
   263	    model, snapshot = r2_load.load_base(name)
   264	    layer = U.spec(name)["steer_layer"]
   265	    val_tables = None
   266	    if val_n:
   267	        vr = random.Random(M.VAL_SEED)
   268	        val_tables = [pb_pin.draw_table(vr, pool["test"]) for _ in range(val_n)]
   269	    load_seconds = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   270	    log(model=name, snapshot=snapshot, kind=kind, layer=layer, load_s=load_seconds)
   271	    model, steer, rec = _run_once(model, pool, prefix, name, out_dir, steps, traj_len, n_traj, val_tables, lr, kind,
   272	                                  layer)
   273	    chosen = out_dir / "edit" / "chosen"
   274	    _save(model, steer, kind, chosen)
   275	    runs = [rec]
   276	    chosen_i = 0
   277	    if (allow_retry and kind == "e7" and rec["diverged"] and max_seconds
   278	            and (time.time() - t0) * 2.5 < max_seconds):
   279	        log(retry=True, lr=lr * 0.25)
   280	        if steer is not None:
   281	            steer.remove()
   282	        # The first run is already at edit/chosen. A kill during the retry leaves it there.
   283	        atomic_json(out_dir / "train.json", {"runs": runs, "chosen": 0, "complete": False, "retrying": True})
   284	        model, steer2, rec2 = _run_once(model, pool, prefix, name, out_dir / "retry", steps, traj_len, n_traj,
   285	                                        val_tables, lr * 0.25, kind, layer)
   286	        runs.append(rec2)
   287	
   288	        def key(r):
   289	            final = r["val"][str(steps)]["pin"]
   290	            return final["accepted"], final["rows_right"]
   291	
   292	        if key(rec2) > key(rec):
   293	            _save(model, steer2, kind, chosen)
   294	            chosen_i = 1
   295	        if steer2 is not None and chosen_i == 0:
   296	            steer2.remove()
   297	    elif rec["diverged"] and kind == "e7" and allow_retry:
   298	        rec["retry_skipped"] = "not enough max_seconds left for a second run"
   299	        log(retry_skipped=True)
   300	    record = {"model": name, "snapshot": snapshot, "kind": kind, "lr": lr, "steps": steps, "traj_len": traj_len,
   301	              "n_traj": n_traj, "supervised_per_step": n_traj * traj_len, "val_n": val_n, "runs": runs,
   302	              "chosen": chosen_i, "host": host, "load_seconds": load_seconds}
   303	    _finish_record(runs[chosen_i], kind, chosen, host, name)
   304	    record["sha256"] = runs[chosen_i]["sha256"]
   305	    record["complete"] = True
   306	    record["at"] = stamp()
   307	    record["wall"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   308	    atomic_json(out_dir / "train.json", record)
   309	    log(train_done=name, sha256=record["sha256"], wall=record["wall"], diverged=[r["diverged"] for r in runs],
   310	        chosen=chosen_i)
   311	    del model
   312	    gc.collect()
   313	    if torch.cuda.is_available():
   314	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   315	    return record
   316	
   317	
   318	def main():
   319	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   320	    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
   321	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   322	    ap.add_argument("--max-seconds", type=int, default=0)
   323	    a = ap.parse_args()
   324	    if a.model not in M.MODELS:
   325	        raise SystemExit(f"{a.model} is not one of the four")
   326	    # The counted entry point cannot silently change the recipe.
   327	    train_edit(a.model, a.out, M.STEPS, M.TRAJ_LEN, M.N_TRAJ, M.VAL_N, a.max_seconds, allow_retry=True)
   328	
   329	
   330	if __name__ == "__main__":
   331	    main()
     1	"""
     2	History-format training examples.
     3	
     4	A trajectory is the true run: Π, then s0 e0 s1 e1 ... with s_{k+1} = δ(s_k, e_k).
     5	Positions are 0, 1, 2, ... through Π and the run. Attention is causal, so each
     6	event sees Π and the whole run so far. Loss sits on the event tokens.
     7	
     8	This is the remember policy's presentation. UNIVERSAL's packed blocks reset
     9	every block to positions |Π| and |Π|+1 and hide the blocks from each other.
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import pathlib
    15	import sys
    16	
    17	_HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    18	for _rel in (".", "../promptbox"):
    19	    _p = (_HERE / _rel).resolve()
    20	    if _p.is_dir() and str(_p) not in sys.path:
    21	        sys.path.insert(0, str(_p))
    22	
    23	import pb_pin  # noqa: E402
    24	
    25	
    26	def rollout(rng, table, pool, length):
    27	    io, nl = pool["id_of"], pool["nl"]
    28	    delta = {(s, e): t for s, e, t in table["rows"]}
    29	    s = table["start"]
    30	    blocks, labels = [], []
    31	    for _ in range(length):
    32	        e = rng.choice(table["events"])
    33	        nxt = delta[(s, e)]
    34	        blocks.append((io[s], io[e]))
    35	        labels.append(io[nxt])
    36	        s = nxt
    37	    return {"pi": pb_pin.encode_table(table, io, nl), "blocks": blocks, "labels": labels,
    38	            "rows": len(table["rows"]), "start": io[table["start"]]}
    39	
    40	
    41	def history_example(rng, pool, length, split="train"):
    42	    return rollout(rng, pb_pin.draw_table(rng, pool[split]), pool, length)
    43	
    44	
    45	def layout(ex):
    46	    """Python view of one example, before padding. Positions continue from 0."""
    47	    pi = list(ex["pi"])
    48	    P = len(pi)
    49	    ids = list(pi)
    50	    steer = [0] * P
    51	    lab = []
    52	    for (s, e) in ex["blocks"]:
    53	        ids.append(int(s))
    54	        ids.append(int(e))
    55	        steer.append(1)
    56	        steer.append(1)
    57	        lab.append(len(ids) - 1)
    58	    if lab != [P + 2 * i + 1 for i in range(len(ex["blocks"]))]:
    59	        raise RuntimeError("event positions are not the second token of each tick")
    60	    return {"ids": ids, "pos": list(range(len(ids))), "steer": steer, "lab": lab, "P": P,
    61	            "labels": [int(x) for x in ex["labels"]]}
    62	
    63	
    64	def history_tensors(examples):
    65	    """Pad a list of history examples. Causal visibility, steer on the stream only."""
    66	    import torch
    67	    laid = [layout(ex) for ex in examples]
    68	    L = max(len(one["ids"]) for one in laid)
    69	    B = len(laid)
    70	    ids = torch.full((B, L), 0, dtype=torch.long)
    71	    pos = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.long)
    72	    allow = torch.zeros((B, 1, L, L), dtype=torch.bool)
    73	    steer = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.bool)
    74	    rows, cols, labels = [], [], []
    75	    stats = []
    76	    for i, one in enumerate(laid):
    77	        n = len(one["ids"])
    78	        P = one["P"]
    79	        ids[i, :n] = torch.tensor(one["ids"])
    80	        pos[i, :n] = torch.tensor(one["pos"])
    81	        idx = torch.arange(n)
    82	        allow[i, 0, :n, :n] = idx[:, None] >= idx[None, :]
    83	        if n < L:
    84	            pad = torch.arange(n, L)
    85	            allow[i, 0, pad, pad] = True
    86	        steer[i, P:n] = True
    87	        for p, lab in zip(one["lab"], one["labels"]):
    88	            rows.append(i)
    89	            cols.append(p)
    90	            labels.append(lab)
    91	        stats.append({
    92	            "pi_len": P, "seq_len": n, "last_pos": int(one["pos"][-1]), "n_labels": len(one["labels"]),
    93	            "steer_on_pi": int(sum(one["steer"][:P])), "steer_on_stream": int(sum(one["steer"][P:])),
    94	            "traj_len": len(one["labels"]),
    95	        })
    96	    return (ids, pos, allow, torch.tensor(rows), torch.tensor(cols), torch.tensor(labels), steer, stats)
    97	
    98	
    99	def decimate(pairs, first_step, cap=96):
   100	    """Keep the shape of a margin trace without storing every tick."""
   101	    if not pairs:
   102	        return []
   103	    pairs = [(int(step), float(margin)) for step, margin in pairs]
   104	    if len(pairs) <= cap:
   105	        keep = list(pairs)
   106	    else:
   107	        stride = (len(pairs) + cap - 1) // cap
   108	        keep = list(pairs[::stride])
   109	        if keep[-1][0] != pairs[-1][0]:
   110	            keep.append(pairs[-1])
   111	        if keep[0][0] != pairs[0][0]:
   112	            keep.insert(0, pairs[0])
   113	    if first_step is not None:
   114	        hit = next((pair for pair in pairs if pair[0] == int(first_step)), None)
   115	        if hit is not None and all(pair[0] != hit[0] for pair in keep):
   116	            keep.append(hit)
   117	    ordered = sorted({pair[0]: pair for pair in keep}.values())
   118	    return [[step, round(margin, 4)] for step, margin in ordered]
   119	
   120	
   121	def presentation_ok(stat, traj_len):
   122	    """True when this example is a continuing causal trajectory of `traj_len` events."""
   123	    return (stat["n_labels"] == traj_len
   124	            and stat["traj_len"] == traj_len
   125	            and stat["last_pos"] == stat["seq_len"] - 1
   126	            and stat["last_pos"] == stat["pi_len"] + 2 * traj_len - 1
   127	            and stat["steer_on_pi"] == 0
   128	            and stat["steer_on_stream"] == 2 * traj_len)

     1	"""
     2	One model, one fresh load of its UNIVERSAL edit, three cache policies.
     3	
     4	    pin       bi_stream PIN: pb_pin.tick. Π kept, the block computed on a clone and discarded,
     5	              positions |Π| and |Π|+1.
     6	    remember  bi_stream FULL: every past (state, event) token kept, positions continuing.
     7	    window    bi_stream SW4b: StreamingLLM. Π is the sink, re-rotated to sit just before the
     8	              last 3 stream tokens (k = 4, the middle of H2b's 2, 4, 8).
     9	
    10	The policy bodies are bi_stream's. Two adaptations, both recorded in the job file:
    11	    rotate_kv is the same extra rotation, done with that model's own rotate_half, and only on
    12	    the rotary subspace when the model rotates part of the head (Phi-4). Where the rotary
    13	    module's attention_scaling is not 1, the increment uses the unscaled angles, so the scale
    14	    is not applied twice. A check that R(d) R(p) = R(p+d) against the model's own apply must
    15	    pass before any tape. On Qwen3 the run uses bi_stream's rotation unchanged, and the check
    16	    requires the two to be bitwise equal.
    17	    On a sliding-window cache layer, only the last sliding_window-1 of the keys this policy
    18	    selected are installed, and cumulative_length is that count. That is the cache the layer
    19	    keeps in its own forward. Full-attention layers receive every selected key.
    20	
    21	LoRA is loaded unmerged. A bf16 merge can drop a rank-1 delta (bi_edit.py); UNIVERSAL's G1 ran unmerged.
    22	"""
    23	
    24	from __future__ import annotations
    25	
    26	import argparse
    27	import hashlib
    28	import importlib
    29	import json
    30	import pathlib
    31	import random
    32	import sys
    33	import time
    34	
    35	sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
    36	
    37	import bi_stream  # noqa: E402
    38	import pb_pin  # noqa: E402
    39	import rem_models as M  # noqa: E402
    40	import uni_models as U  # noqa: E402
    41	from pb_common import atomic_json, host_record, stamp  # noqa: E402
    42	
    43	_BI_ROTATE = bi_stream.rotate_kv
    44	_BI_CACHE = bi_stream.cache_from
    45	_ROT = {"max_delta": 0}
    46	
    47	
    48	def _cos_sin(rot, delta, like):
    49	    import torch
    50	    pos = torch.tensor([[int(delta)]], device=like.device)
    51	    try:
    52	        return rot(torch.zeros(1, dtype=torch.float32, device=like.device), pos)
    53	    except Exception:
    54	        return rot(like, pos)
    55	
    56	
    57	def rotate_impl(model, kvs, delta):
    58	    """R(delta) on cached post-RoPE keys. Matches bi_stream.rotate_kv on a full-head RoPE,
    59	    and rotates only the rotary subspace when cos is shorter than the head (partial RoPE)."""
    60	    import torch
    61	    if delta == 0 or not kvs:
    62	        return kvs
    63	    c = U.core(model)
    64	    mod = importlib.import_module(type(c).__module__)
    65	    rotate_half = mod.rotate_half
    66	    rot = c.model.rotary_emb
    67	    cos, sin = _cos_sin(rot, delta, kvs[0][0])
    68	    cos, sin = cos.float(), sin.float()
    69	    if cos.ndim == 2:  # (seq, dim) -> (1, seq, dim)
    70	        cos, sin = cos[None], sin[None]
    71	    if cos.ndim == 3:  # (batch, seq, dim) -> (batch, 1, seq, dim), as bi_stream
    72	        cos, sin = cos[:, None], sin[:, None]
    73	    scale = getattr(rot, "attention_scaling", 1.0)
    74	    try:
    75	        scale = float(scale)
    76	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    77	        scale = 1.0
    78	    # A scaled rotary embedding multiplies cos and sin by attention_scaling. The cached key
    79	    # was already scaled once; the increment has to be a pure rotation or the scale is applied twice.
    80	    if scale != 1.0:
    81	        cos, sin = cos / scale, sin / scale
    82	    rotary_dim = cos.shape[-1]
    83	    out = []
    84	    for k, v in kvs:
    85	        kf = k.float()
    86	        if rotary_dim == kf.shape[-1]:
    87	            kr = kf * cos + rotate_half(kf) * sin
    88	        elif rotary_dim < kf.shape[-1]:
    89	            head, tail = kf[..., :rotary_dim], kf[..., rotary_dim:]
    90	            kr = torch.cat((head * cos + rotate_half(head) * sin, tail), -1)
    91	        else:
    92	            raise RuntimeError(f"rotary dim {rotary_dim} exceeds head {kf.shape[-1]}")
    93	        out.append((kr.to(dtype=k.dtype), v))
    94	    return out
    95	
    96	
    97	def rotate_kv(model, kvs, delta):
    98	    _ROT["max_delta"] = max(_ROT["max_delta"], int(delta))
    99	    # Qwen3 uses bi_stream's rotation unchanged. Other models use the same math with their rotate_half.
   100	    if type(U.core(model)).__name__.startswith("Qwen3"):
   101	        return _BI_ROTATE(model, kvs, delta)
   102	    return rotate_impl(model, kvs, delta)
   103	
   104	
   105	def cache_from(template, kvs):
   106	    """bi_stream's cache, then: a sliding layer is given only the last sliding_window-1 of the keys
   107	    the policy selected, and its cumulative_length is that count. That is the cache the layer keeps
   108	    in its own forward. Full-attention layers receive every selected key."""
   109	    new = _BI_CACHE(template, kvs)
   110	    for layer in new.layers:
   111	        if not getattr(layer, "is_sliding", False) or getattr(layer, "keys", None) is None:
   112	            continue
   113	        w = int(layer.sliding_window)
   114	        if layer.keys.shape[-2] > w - 1:
   115	            layer.keys = layer.keys[..., -(w - 1):, :].contiguous()
   116	            layer.values = layer.values[..., -(w - 1):, :].contiguous()
   117	        layer.cumulative_length = int(layer.keys.shape[-2])
   118	        if hasattr(layer, "is_initialized"):
   119	            layer.is_initialized = True
   120	    return new
   121	
   122	
   123	def install_patches():
   124	    bi_stream.rotate_kv = rotate_kv
   125	    bi_stream.cache_from = cache_from
   126	
   127	
   128	def rope_check(model):
   129	    """max |R(d) R(p) x - R(p+d) x| using the model's own apply at p and at p+d,
   130	    and rotate_impl for the increment. Also, on Qwen3, rotate_impl against bi_stream's original."""
   131	    import torch
   132	    c = U.core(model)
   133	    mod = importlib.import_module(type(c).__module__)
   134	    apply = mod.apply_rotary_pos_emb
   135	    rot = c.model.rotary_emb
   136	    hd = getattr(c.config, "head_dim", None) or c.config.hidden_size // c.config.num_attention_heads
   137	    device = next(c.parameters()).device
   138	    x = torch.randn(1, 1, 4, hd, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
   139	
   140	    def at(pos):
   141	        cos, sin = _cos_sin(rot, pos, x)
   142	        try:
   143	            return apply(x, x, cos, sin)[1]
   144	        except (RuntimeError, IndexError):
   145	            rd = int(cos.shape[-1])
   146	            head, tail = x[..., :rd], x[..., rd:]
   147	            return torch.cat((apply(head, head, cos, sin)[1], tail), -1)
   148	
   149	    p, d = 17, 5
   150	    err = float((rotate_impl(model, [(at(p), at(p))], d)[0][0].float() - at(p + d).float()).abs().max())
   151	    qwen = None
   152	    if type(c).__name__.startswith("Qwen3"):
   153	        k = torch.randn(1, c.config.num_key_value_heads, 6, hd, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
   154	        a = rotate_impl(model, [(k, k)], d)[0][0]
   155	        b = _BI_ROTATE(model, [(k, k)], d)[0][0]
   156	        qwen = float((a.float() - b.float()).abs().max())
   157	        if qwen != 0.0:
   158	            raise RuntimeError(f"rotate_kv disagrees with bi_stream on Qwen3 by {qwen}")
   159	    elif err > 1e-3:
   160	        raise RuntimeError(f"RoPE does not compose: max abs {err}")
   161	    scale = getattr(rot, "attention_scaling", 1.0)
   162	    try:
   163	        scale = float(scale)
   164	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   165	        scale = None
   166	    return {"max_abs": err, "qwen_max_abs": qwen, "head_dim": hd, "attention_scaling": scale}
   167	
   168	
   169	def file_sha256(path):
   170	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   171	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   172	        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
   173	            h.update(chunk)
   174	    return h.hexdigest()
   175	
   176	
   177	def hub_snapshot(repo, revision=None):
   178	    """The snapshot directory on the read-only model volume. Reads refs only; writes nothing."""
   179	    root = pathlib.Path("/hf/hub") / ("models--" + repo.replace("/", "--"))
   180	    rev = revision or "main"
   181	    ref = root / "refs" / rev
   182	    commit = ref.read_text().strip() if ref.is_file() else rev
   183	    snap = root / "snapshots" / commit
   184	    if (snap / "config.json").is_file():
   185	        return snap
   186	    alt = root / "snapshots" / rev
   187	    if (alt / "config.json").is_file():
   188	        return alt
   189	    kids = sorted(p.name for p in root.iterdir())[:30] if root.is_dir() else []
   190	    raise FileNotFoundError(f"no snapshot for {repo}@{rev}; root={root} exists={root.is_dir()} entries={kids}")
   191	
   192	
   193	def load_fresh(name):
   194	    """bf16, the attention implementation UNIVERSAL used, from the local snapshot. No hub write."""
   195	    import torch
   196	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
   197	    s = U.spec(name)
   198	    path = hub_snapshot(s["repo"], s.get("revision"))
   199	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(path), dtype=torch.bfloat16,
   200	                                                 attn_implementation=s["attn"], local_files_only=True).cuda()
   201	    model.eval()
   202	    return model, str(path)
   203	
   204	
   205	def tokenizer_fresh(name):
   206	    from tokenizers import Tokenizer
   207	    s = U.spec(name)
   208	    return Tokenizer.from_file(str(hub_snapshot(s["repo"], s.get("revision")) / "tokenizer.json"))
   209	
   210	
   211	def load_edit(model, spec):
   212	    """Fresh load of the counted checkpoint. LoRA stays unmerged."""
   213	    path = pathlib.Path(spec["path"])
   214	    if spec["kind"] == "e7":
   215	        import torch
   216	        weight = path / "steer.pt"
   217	        d = torch.load(weight, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
   218	        steer = U.Steer(model, d["layer"], d["where"], d["vec"])
   219	        undo = U.block_only_prefill(steer)
   220	        return steer, undo, {"kind": "e7", "path": spec["path"], "sha256": file_sha256(weight),
   221	                             "layer": int(d["layer"]), "where": d["where"]}
   222	    from peft import PeftModel
   223	    weight = path / "adapter_model.safetensors"
   224	    if not weight.exists():
   225	        cands = list(path.glob("adapter_model.*"))
   226	        if not cands:
   227	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"no adapter weights in {path}")
   228	        weight = cands[0]
   229	    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, str(path))
   230	    model.eval()
   231	    return model, (lambda: None), {"kind": "e3", "path": spec["path"], "sha256": file_sha256(weight),
   232	                                   "merged": False}
   233	
   234	
   235	def layer_summary(model, past):
   236	    cfg = U.core(model).config
   237	    types = list(getattr(cfg, "layer_types", []) or [])
   238	    return {"sliding_window": getattr(cfg, "sliding_window", None),
   239	            "n_sliding_layers": sum(1 for layer in past.layers if getattr(layer, "is_sliding", False)),
   240	            "n_full_layers": sum(1 for layer in past.layers if not getattr(layer, "is_sliding", False)),
   241	            "layer_types": {t: types.count(t) for t in sorted(set(types))}}
   242	
   243	
   244	def classify(nxt, s, e, delta, state_ids, event_ids, nl):
   245	    """BRIDGE / PROMPTBOX split, on a tick whose event was streamed in.
   246	    step: the argmax is a state of the table, and not δ(s, e).
   247	    reading: the argmax is not a state (an event, the newline, or some other token)."""
   248	    if nxt in state_ids:
   249	        kind, akind = "step", "state"
   250	    elif nxt in event_ids:
   251	        kind, akind = "reading", "event"
   252	    elif nxt == nl:
   253	        kind, akind = "reading", "newline"
   254	    else:
   255	        kind, akind = "reading", "other"
   256	    other = False
   257	    if akind == "state":
   258	        other = any(delta.get((s2, e)) == nxt and s2 != s for s2 in state_ids)
   259	    return kind, akind, other
   260	
   261	
   262	def run_one(model, pi, policy_code, s0, tape, want, delta, cert_logits, state_ids, event_ids, nl, stop_early):
   263	    import torch
   264	    _ROT["max_delta"] = 0
   265	    past = pb_pin.prefill(model, pi)
   266	    P = len(pi)
   267	    r = bi_stream.Run(model, past, P, policy_code)
   268	    s, got = s0, []
   269	    bitwise = off = 0
   270	    first = kind = akind = None
   271	    other = copy_state = False
   272	    t0 = time.time()
   273	    for i, e in enumerate(tape, start=1):
   274	        lg = r.tick(i, s, e)
   275	        nxt = pb_pin.top2(lg)[0]
   276	        if policy_code == "PIN":
   277	            if (s, e) not in cert_logits:
   278	                off += 1
   279	            elif torch.equal(lg.detach(), cert_logits[(s, e)]):
   280	                bitwise += 1
   281	        got.append(nxt)
   282	        if first is None and nxt != want[i - 1]:
   283	            first = i - 1
   284	            kind, akind, other = classify(nxt, s, e, delta, state_ids, event_ids, nl)
   285	            copy_state = nxt == s
   286	            if stop_early:
   287	                break
   288	        s = nxt
   289	        if i < len(tape):
   290	            r.feed_state(s)
   291	        if policy_code == "PIN" and i % 512 == 0:
   292	            print(json.dumps({"pin_tick": i, "bitwise": bitwise}), flush=True)
   293	    ticks = len(got)
   294	    if policy_code == "PIN":
   295	        if r.j != 0 or _ROT["max_delta"] != 0:
   296	            raise RuntimeError(f"pin grew the cache: j={r.j} max_delta={_ROT['max_delta']}")
   297	        if ticks != len(tape):
   298	            raise RuntimeError(f"pin stopped at {ticks}")
   299	    else:
   300	        if r.j != 2 * ticks:
   301	            raise RuntimeError(f"{policy_code} stream length j={r.j} ticks={ticks}")
   302	        if _ROT["max_delta"] != 0 and policy_code == "FULL":
   303	            raise RuntimeError("remember re-rotated keys")
   304	        if policy_code.startswith("SW") and ticks >= 4 and _ROT["max_delta"] <= 0:
   305	            raise RuntimeError("window never re-rotated")
   306	    del past, r
   307	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   308	    return {"ticks": ticks, "first_error": first, "first_error_step": None if first is None else first + 1,
   309	            "first_error_kind": kind, "argmax_kind": akind, "other_row_same_event": other, "copy_state": copy_state,
   310	            "pin_bitwise": bitwise, "pin_off_table": off,
   311	            "pin_mismatch": (ticks - bitwise - off) if policy_code == "PIN" else 0,
   312	            "stream_tokens": None if policy_code == "PIN" else 2 * ticks,
   313	            "max_delta": _ROT["max_delta"], "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 2)}
   314	
   315	
   316	def draw_selected(pool, indices):
   317	    rng = random.Random(M.TABLE_SEED)
   318	    tables = [pb_pin.draw_table(rng, pool["test"]) for _ in range(128)]
   319	    return {i: tables[i] for i in indices}
   320	
   321	
   322	def save_all(path, payload):
   323	    atomic_json(path, payload)
   324	    # the recount reads this csv; it is written from the same payload as the json
   325	    lines = ["cohort,model,table,rows,bin,shape,tape,policy,length,T,exact,first_error_step,kind,argmax_kind,"
   326	             "pin_bitwise,pin_ticks,pin_off_table,cert_pass,stream_tokens,max_delta,copy_state,other_row"]
   327	    for run in payload["runs"]:
   328	        for L, exact in run["exact_at"].items():
   329	            full = run["policy"] == "pin" and int(L) == run["T"]
   330	            lines.append(",".join(str(x) for x in (
   331	                payload["cohort"], payload["model"], run["table"], run["rows"], run["bin"], run["shape"],
   332	                run["tape"], run["policy"], L, run["T"], int(exact),
   333	                "" if run["first_error_step"] is None else run["first_error_step"],
   334	                run["first_error_kind"] or "", run["argmax_kind"] or "",
   335	                run["pin_bitwise"] if full else "", run["ticks"] if full else "",
   336	                run["pin_off_table"] if full else "", int(run["cert_pass"]),
   337	                "" if run["stream_tokens"] is None else run["stream_tokens"], run["max_delta"],
   338	                int(bool(run["copy_state"])), int(bool(run["other_row_same_event"])))))
   339	    csv_path = path.with_name("runs.csv")
   340	    csv_path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
   341	
   342	
   343	def run(a, out):
   344	    import torch
   345	    torch.set_grad_enabled(False)
   346	    install_patches()
   347	    spec = M.EDITS[a.model]
   348	    fingers = {fp[0]: fp for fp in M.TABLES[a.model]}
   349	    indices = [int(x) for x in a.tables.split(",")] if a.tables else M.table_indices(a.model)
   350	    for i in indices:
   351	        if i not in fingers:
   352	            raise SystemExit(f"table {i} is not in the registry for {a.model}")
   353	    lengths = [int(x) for x in a.lengths.split(",")]
   354	    T = max(lengths)
   355	    policies = a.policies.split(",")
   356	    if "window" in policies and a.window_k != M.WINDOW_K:
   357	        raise SystemExit(f"window k is {M.WINDOW_K}")
   358	    s = U.spec(a.model)
   359	    t0 = time.time()
   360	    tok = tokenizer_fresh(a.model)
   361	    pool = U.name_pool(tok)
   362	    prefix = U.doc_prefix(tok)
   363	    print(json.dumps({"pool_test": len(pool["test"]), "prefix": len(prefix)}), flush=True)
   364	    model, snapshot = load_fresh(a.model)
   365	    loaded, undo, edit = load_edit(model, spec)
   366	    if spec["kind"] == "e3":
   367	        model = loaded
   368	    rope = rope_check(model)
   369	    print(json.dumps({"model": a.model, "snapshot": snapshot, "edit": edit, "rope": rope,
   370	                      "load_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1)}), flush=True)
   371	    tables = draw_selected(pool, indices)
   372	    io, nl = pool["id_of"], pool["nl"]
   373	    # one prefill to read the cache layer types; its cache is not reused
   374	    probe_tb = tables[indices[0]]
   375	    probe_pi = list(prefix) + pb_pin.encode_table(probe_tb, io, nl)
   376	    probe_past = pb_pin.prefill(model, probe_pi)
   377	    layers = layer_summary(model, probe_past)
   378	    del probe_past
   379	    host = host_record()
   380	    import transformers
   381	    payload = {"model": a.model, "cohort": spec["cohort"], "snapshot": snapshot, "edit": edit, "rope_check": rope,
   382	               "layers": layers, "transformers": transformers.__version__,
   383	               "seed": a.seed, "table_seed": M.TABLE_SEED, "lengths": lengths, "tapes": a.tapes, "T": T,
   384	               "policies": policies, "window_k": M.WINDOW_K if "window" in policies else None,
   385	               "window_code": f"SW{M.WINDOW_K}b" if "window" in policies else None,
   386	               "host": host, "torch": torch.__version__, "at": stamp(), "runs": [], "tables": [], "complete": False}
   387	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   388	    path = out / "runs.json"
   389	    for i in indices:
   390	        tb = tables[i]
   391	        fp = fingers[i]
   392	        if (len(tb["rows"]), len(tb["states"]), len(tb["events"]), tb["shape"]) != (fp[1], fp[2], fp[3], fp[4]):
   393	            raise RuntimeError(f"table {i} structure {len(tb['rows'])} {tb['shape']} != registry {fp}")
   394	        pi = list(prefix) + pb_pin.encode_table(tb, io, nl)
   395	        rows = [(io[s_], io[e_], io[t_]) for s_, e_, t_ in tb["rows"]]
   396	        cert, past, logits = pb_pin.certify(model, pi, rows, keep_logits=True)
   397	        del past
   398	        logits = {k: v.detach() for k, v in logits.items()}
   399	        payload["tables"].append({"i": i, "rows": fp[1], "n": fp[2], "a": fp[3], "shape": fp[4], "bin": fp[5],
   400	                                  "expect_pass": fp[6], "cert_pass": cert["pass"], "rows_wrong": cert["rows_wrong"],
   401	                                  "min_margin": cert["min_margin"]})
   402	        save_all(path, payload)
   403	        print(json.dumps(payload["tables"][-1]), flush=True)
   404	        if spec["cohort"] == "main" and not cert["pass"]:
   405	            raise RuntimeError(f"table {i} failed re-certification ({cert['rows_wrong']} rows wrong); "
   406	                               f"not the function UNIVERSAL certified")
   407	        if spec["cohort"] == "olmo" and fp[6] and not cert["pass"]:
   408	            raise RuntimeError(f"OLMo table {i} was certified by UNIVERSAL and failed re-certification")
   409	        delta = {(io[s_], io[e_]): io[t_] for s_, e_, t_ in tb["rows"]}
   410	        state_ids = {io[s_] for s_ in tb["states"]}
   411	        event_ids = {io[e_] for e_ in tb["events"]}
   412	        s0 = io[tb["start"]]
   413	        for tape_k, idxs in enumerate(M.event_tapes(len(tb["events"]), i, n=a.tapes, T=T, seed=a.seed)):
   414	            tape = [io[tb["events"][j]] for j in idxs]
   415	            want, s_true = [], s0
   416	            for e in tape:
   417	                s_true = delta[(s_true, e)]
   418	                want.append(s_true)
   419	            for policy in policies:
   420	                code = {"pin": "PIN", "remember": "FULL", "window": f"SW{M.WINDOW_K}b"}[policy]
   421	                rec = run_one(model, pi, code, s0, tape, want, delta, logits, state_ids, event_ids, nl,
   422	                              stop_early=(policy != "pin"))
   423	                exact = {str(L): M.exact_at(rec["first_error"], L) for L in lengths}
   424	                if rec["first_error"] is None and rec["ticks"] != T:
   425	                    raise RuntimeError("exact run did not finish the tape")
   426	                row = {"table": i, "rows": fp[1], "bin": fp[5], "shape": fp[4], "tape": tape_k, "policy": policy,
   427	                       "T": T, "cert_pass": cert["pass"], "exact_at": exact, **rec}
   428	                payload["runs"].append(row)
   429	                print(json.dumps({k: row[k] for k in ("table", "tape", "policy", "exact_at", "first_error_step",
   430	                                                      "first_error_kind", "argmax_kind", "pin_bitwise", "ticks",
   431	                                                      "max_delta", "seconds")}), flush=True)
   432	                save_all(path, payload)
   433	        del logits
   434	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   435	    payload["complete"] = True
   436	    payload["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   437	    payload["at"] = stamp()
   438	    save_all(path, payload)
   439	    if spec["kind"] == "e7":
   440	        loaded.remove()
   441	    undo()
   442	    print(json.dumps({"complete": True, "runs": len(payload["runs"]), "seconds": payload["seconds"]}), flush=True)
   443	
   444	
   445	def main():
   446	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   447	    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
   448	    ap.add_argument("--tables", default="")
   449	    ap.add_argument("--lengths", default="256,1024,4096")
   450	    ap.add_argument("--tapes", type=int, default=M.TAPES)
   451	    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=M.SEED)
   452	    ap.add_argument("--policies", default="pin,remember,window")
   453	    ap.add_argument("--window_k", type=int, default=M.WINDOW_K)
   454	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   455	    a = ap.parse_args()
   456	    run(a, pathlib.Path(a.out))
   457	
   458	
   459	if __name__ == "__main__":
   460	    main()
     1	"""
     2	REMEMBER registry: which edit, which tables, which tapes.
     3	
     4	The 128 G1 tables are seed 777, the same draw as UNIVERSAL. A model's tables are the ones its counted
     5	G1 file already certified, spread over the size bins by the rule in REMEMBER.md. The OLMo pretraining
     6	arm uses two table indices, the same ones at every checkpoint. Tapes are event indices into that table,
     7	so the same index sequence is the same tape in every tokenizer.
     8	
     9	Nothing here loads a model. `python rem_models.py` checks the registry against the local G1 files.
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import json
    15	import pathlib
    16	import random
    17	
    18	SEED = 20261028
    19	TABLE_SEED = 777
    20	WINDOW_K = 4  # middle of H2b's 2, 4, 8; bi_stream policy SW4b
    21	LENGTHS = (256, 1024, 4096)
    22	TAPES = 4
    23	POLICIES = ("pin", "remember", "window")
    24	KEY_LENGTH = 4096  # the length the three recounted numbers use, unless results/plan.json amends it
    25	# Smoke, before any counted job. Tables are members of TABLES. Length 64, one tape, all three policies.
    26	SMOKE_LENGTH, SMOKE_TAPES = 64, 1
    27	SMOKE = (

   100	
   101	def table_indices(name):
   102	    return [t[0] for t in TABLES[name]]
   103	
   104	
   105	def exact_at(first_error, length):
   106	    """A prefix of `length` events is exact when the first mismatch is at that index or later.
   107	    `first_error` is the 0-based event index, or None."""
   108	    return first_error is None or first_error >= length
   109	
   110	
   111	def lower_median(values):
   112	    """Element at index (n-1)//2 of the sorted values. None if empty."""
   113	    xs = sorted(values)
   114	    if not xs:
   115	        return None
   116	    return xs[(len(xs) - 1) // 2]
   117	
   118	
   119	def event_tapes(n_events, table_i, n=TAPES, T=4096, seed=SEED):
   120	    """`n` tapes of `T` event indices. The generator is Random(seed + table_i * 100003),
   121	    so tape k of table i does not depend on which model or which other table was drawn."""
   122	    rng = random.Random(seed + table_i * 100003)
   123	    return [[rng.randrange(n_events) for _ in range(T)] for _ in range(n)]
   124	
   125	
   126	def select_main(records):
   127	    """Lowest index in each nonempty size bin, then lowest remaining indices, until 4."""
   128	    passed = [t for t in records if t["pass"]]
   129	    chosen = []
   130	    for b in BINS:
   131	        cands = [t for t in passed if t["bin"] == b and t["i"] not in {c["i"] for c in chosen}]
   132	        if cands:
   133	            chosen.append(min(cands, key=lambda t: t["i"]))
   134	    rest = sorted((t for t in passed if t["i"] not in {c["i"] for c in chosen}), key=lambda t: t["i"])
   135	    while len(chosen) < 4 and rest:
   136	        chosen.append(rest.pop(0))
   137	    return tuple(sorted(chosen, key=lambda t: t["i"]))
   138	
   139	
   140	def finger(t):
   141	    return (t["i"], t["rows"], t["n"], t["a"], t["shape"], t["bin"], bool(t["pass"]))
   142	
   143	
   144	def verify(results_root):
   145	    """The hardcoded registry against the G1 files and the rules. Raises AssertionError."""
   146	    root = pathlib.Path(results_root)
   147	    loaded = {}
   148	    for name, spec in EDITS.items():
   149	        recs = json.loads((root / spec["g1"]).read_text())["tables"]
   150	        loaded[name] = recs
   151	        if spec["cohort"] == "main":
   152	            got = tuple(finger(t) for t in select_main(recs))
   153	            assert got == TABLES[name], (name, got, TABLES[name])
   154	        for fp in TABLES[name]:
   155	            t = next(x for x in recs if x["i"] == fp[0])
   156	            assert finger(t) == fp, (name, finger(t), fp)
   157	    # OLMo: two lowest indices certified by both 84B and the released base
   158	    def passed(name):
   159	        return {t["i"] for t in loaded[name] if t["pass"]}
   160	    shared = sorted(passed("olmo2-7b-84B") & passed("olmo2-7b-base"))
   161	    assert shared[:2] == [29, 45], shared
   162	    assert 29 not in passed("olmo2-7b-5B") and 45 not in passed("olmo2-7b-5B")
   163	    # tapes: same indices for the same table, independent of call order
   164	    assert event_tapes(5, 8, n=2, T=4) == event_tapes(5, 8, n=2, T=4)
   165	    assert event_tapes(5, 8, n=1, T=3) != event_tapes(5, 9, n=1, T=3)
   166	    assert exact_at(None, 256) and exact_at(256, 256) and not exact_at(255, 256)
   167	    assert lower_median([4, 1, 9]) == 4 and lower_median([2, 8]) == 2
   168	    for spec in SMOKE:
   169	        idx = int(spec["tables"])
   170	        assert idx in table_indices(spec["model"]), spec
   171	        assert spec["gpu"] == EDITS[spec["model"]]["gpu"], spec
   172	    return True
   173	
   174	
   175	if __name__ == "__main__":
   176	    here = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
   177	    verify(here.parent / "universal" / "results")
   178	    print(json.dumps({"ok": True, "models": len(TABLES), "main_tables": {n: table_indices(n) for n in MAIN},
   179	                      "olmo_tables": table_indices("olmo2-7b-base")}))
