    11	<!-- /SUMMARY -->
    12	
    13	## Question
    14	
    15	REMEMBER found pin trace-exact on 80 of 80 runs at 2048 events, and the same weights with the history kept failed at a median first error of step 3. A referee can say that those edits were learned in pinned format, so the failure is a format shift. This run trains the same edit class on the remember presentation and measures both edits under both policies as the tape grows.
    16	
    17	## Protocol
    18	
    19	Four models: Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B. Two edits each.
    20	
    21	E-pin is the checkpoint REMEMBER measured. It is not retrained. Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B were measured as an E7 vector, and UNIVERSAL did not train an E3 for them. Reusing the edit means not training a new pinned LoRA. E-hist is a new edit of that same class: E7 for both Qwen models, rank-1 LoRA for Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B. A Qwen LoRA beside an E7 pin would mix the format with the parameterization. Paths and the E-pin sha256 values are `pin_path` and `PIN_SHA` in `r2_models.py`. The eval aborts if the E-pin file does not match.
    22	
    23	E-hist uses UNIVERSAL's optimizer and schedule. 100 steps, warmup 5, AdamW betas (0.9, 0.99), weight decay 0, gradient clip 1.0, seed 1. The data RNG is `Random(1 + 7919)`. Learning rate 2e-2 for E7 and 2e-3 for E3. E7 may retry once at a quarter of the learning rate when the run diverged and the elapsed time times 2.5 is still under max_seconds. Diverged means a non-finite loss, or the mean accuracy of the last 10 steps more than 0.02 below the mean of steps 21 through 30. The first checkpoint is written to `edit/chosen` before the retry, so a kill leaves it. The retry replaces it only when the retry's pinned validation accepts more tables, then more rows. E3 does not retry. The saved edit is step 100 unless that retry wins.
    24	
    25	Validation is 128 tables from `Random(101)` on the held-out name pool, scored with UNIVERSAL's packed fast eval at steps 10, 30, and 100, plus 4 teacher-forced history trajectories. The history score is recorded and is not used to choose the checkpoint. The validation tables are not the REMEMBER tables.
    26	
    27	A UNIVERSAL step supervises 48 × 16 = 768 event positions. E-hist keeps 768 by training on 3 trajectories of 256 events, not on 48 independent pinned blocks. The number of tables in a step drops from 48 to 3, because each trajectory contributes 256 losses. The length is 256, not a mixture. "Up to 256" is that cap. A miss at 256 is then either cross-table generalization, when the training accuracy is high, or a failure to learn the history, when it is low. Both answer Q1.
    28	
    29	The loss is cross-entropy on the true destination, teacher-forced along the true trace. The sequence is Π, including the tokenizer's document prefix, then s0 e0 s1 e1 … with s_{k+1} = δ(s_k, e_k). Positions are 0, 1, 2, … through the whole sequence. They are not reset to |Π| and |Π|+1. Attention is causal: each token sees Π and the run so far, not a block-diagonal mask. Loss is only on the event token, the second token of each tick. The steer mask is 0 on Π and on padding and 1 on the stream tokens. `use_cache` is false. Gradient checkpointing uses `use_reentrant` false, and `enable_input_require_grads` runs before the base is frozen, the same order as UNIVERSAL. The E7 vector is added only on stream tokens and is off during Π's prefill.
    30	
    31	Policies are the ones REMEMBER ran. Pin is `bi_stream` PIN: Π is kept, the block is computed on a clone at positions |Π| and |Π|+1, and the clone is discarded. Remember is FULL: nothing is evicted, positions continue, and the run stops at the first wrong state, before that state is fed. There is no window. The patches are `rem_job`'s: `rotate_kv`, the sliding-window crop, and the rope check before any tape.
    32	
    33	Tables are REMEMBER's four certified tables per model, redrawn at seed 777. Tapes use seed 20261028, the same generator as REMEMBER. Four tapes. Lengths 256, 1024, 4096, and 8192. A shorter length is a prefix of the long tape. The smoke length 64 is a prefix of that same generator. Trace-exactness is against the true machine. `exact_at` is true at L when the first mismatch is at index L or later, or there is none. `first_error` is the 0-based index. The first-error step is 1-based. The lower median is the value at index (n−1)//2 of the sorted values.
    34	
    35	E-pin is re-certified in the same process. A failed table aborts the job. E-hist failing certification is a result for Q3, and the tapes still run. Bitwise equality is `torch.equal` on the bf16 logits against the certificate computed in that process. Pin runs the whole tape. The margin at each recorded length is kept for every run that executed that step, and the full trace is stored decimated to at most 96 points, always including step 1, the last step, and the first-error step.
    36	
    37	LoRA stays unmerged. Each job loads the base and one edit from scratch. Bases come from the read-only snapshot on `polyformer-universal-hf` (`/hf`). E-pin files come from `polyformer-universal-results` (`/uni`) and `polyformer-bigidea-results` (`/bi`). Writes go only to `polyformer-remember2-results` (`/vol`). The hub is offline. Models are not downloaded locally. Results go to `research/remember/results2`, so REMEMBER's `results/plan.json` is left alone.
    38	
    39	Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B use the A10G price class. The Modal function requests `gpu="A10"`, and the ledger field stays `A10G`. Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B use L40S. An out-of-memory failure before a complete result is replaced on the next GPU: L40S for an A10G model, H100 for an L40S model, under a new job id. The amendment records it. Measured claims come from the complete replacement.
    40	
    41	A timeout is not a trace failure. The retry has a new job id and `--carry` of the finished cells. The complete `runs.json` is the file the plan's job id names. An incomplete job is not a counted result.
    42	
    43	The three numbers `r2_report.py` checks against `r2_recount.py` are: how many E-hist remember runs are exact at the training length, 256; the lower median first-error step of the E-hist remember runs that are inexact at the plan's key length, together with how many exact runs were excluded and how many runs there were; and how many E-hist pin runs are exact at that key length. The report reads `runs.json`. The recount reads `runs.csv` and does not import the report. The training length and the key length come from `results2/plan.json`. A train sha256 that does not match the E-hist file the eval loaded is an exit, and that edit is not published.
    44	
    45	## Registered predictions
    46	
    47	These paragraphs stay as written. A smoke amendment is inserted by `r2_smoke_read.py` before any counted job, under Amendments, and only by the rules in that file.
    48	
    49	**E-pin, pin.** Probability 0.95. Every E-pin run under pin is trace-exact at 256, 1024, 4096, and 8192, and every pin tick is bitwise equal to the certificate made in that process. A smoke in which E-pin under pin is not exact, or not bitwise, is a bug. It is not a reason to change this sentence.
    50	
    51	**E-pin, remember.** Probability 0.85 that the lower median first-error step on the inexact E-pin remember runs at 8192 is at most 8. Point median 3. 90% range 2 to 32. Probability 0.80 that fewer than half of the E-pin remember runs are exact at 256.
    52	
    53	**E-hist, remember, at the training length.** Probability 0.65 that, in at least 3 of the 4 models, at least half of that model's E-hist remember runs are exact at 256. Point fraction 0.75. 90% range 0.25 to 1.
    54	
    55	**E-hist, remember, as the tape grows.** Probability 0.70 that the fraction exact at 8192 is strictly below the fraction exact at 256. Probability 0.75 that the lower median first-error step on the inexact E-hist remember runs at 8192 is above 32. Point median 512. 90% range 16 to 4096. The shape this names is drift past the training length, the pattern BRIDGE saw, rather than a failure at step 3.
    56	
    57	**E-hist, pin.** Probability 0.60 that every E-hist pin run is trace-exact at every recorded length. The doubt is that the edit never saw positions reset to |Π| and |Π|+1.

    59	The sentences name 8192 events and 4 tapes. If the cost ladder shortens the tape or the tape count, the measured lines say which length and which n were run. These sentences are not edited.

Rates are the Modal list prices in `r2_modal.py`: H100 0.001097, L40S 0.000542, A10G 0.000306, plus 8 CPU at 0.0000131 per second and 48 GiB at 0.00000222 per second. A reservation is that rate times max_seconds + 30 + 240. Reserved amounts count against the cap until the job settles. Settlement is the same rate times the wall, capped at max_seconds + 30, plus the 240-second startup. The ledger records the GPU at reserve and the GPU and the host CPU at settle.

    90	<!-- /COUNTED-COST -->
    91	
    92	The smoke is one job, `smoke-qwen3-1.7b`, on Qwen3-1.7B, table 8, A10G, max_seconds 1500. It trains 2 steps at the counted batch shape, with no validation and no retry, then evaluates both edits under both policies at length 64 on one tape. It also times 256 pin ticks and 256 remember ticks on E-pin, with remember marks every 32 ticks and no early stop. The 2-step weights are not the counted edit. E-hist's smoke exactness does not amend a prediction.
    93	
    94	Smoke rules, applied by `r2_smoke_read.py` and not by hand. If any E-pin remember run is exact at the smoke length L, the amendment sets the point median to 2L and the 90% range to 2 through 3L. If an E-pin remember first-error step falls outside 2 to 32, the amendment widens the range to include the observed steps. E-pin under pin not exact, not bitwise, a failed E-pin re-certification, or any remember run with `stream_tokens` other than 2 × ticks or `max_delta` other than 0, is a bug: the reader exits 2 and writes nothing.
    95	
    96	`r2_cost.py` reads `timing.json` and walks this ladder until the train reservations and the eval reservations both fit in the cap minus what the ledger has already reserved or settled. It does not drop a model or an edit. It does not drop 256: those cells are prefixes, so dropping them saves no compute.
    97	
    98	1. 4 tapes, long length 8192.
    99	2. Tapes 4 to 2.
   100	3. Long length 8192 to 4096.
   101	4. Tapes 2 to 1.
   102	5. Long length 4096 to 2048. The recorded lengths are then 256, 1024, and 2048.
   103	6. Long length 2048 to 1024.
   104	
   105	The projection, fixed here. Pin seconds per tick is the probe's pin seconds divided by its pin ticks, scaled by (S / 1.7)^0.5 for a model of nominal size S. Remember seconds to reach T ticks are a least-squares fit of a T + b T² to the probe marks. A negative coefficient is set to zero and the other coefficient is refit. Remember seconds scale by S / 1.7 and are at least the pin price of the same ticks. A training step scales by S / 1.7. The first smoke step is the compile step and the last is the steady step. Counted training is the compile step plus 99 steady steps, plus the load, plus a validation budget at each of the three checkpoints: 128 packed tables at a quarter of one trajectory forward, and 4 history trajectories. An eval load scales by (S / 1.7)^0.5, and an eval pays for two loads. E-hist remember is priced as a full tape. E-pin remember is priced as pin ticks up to min(T, max(64, 8 times the greatest smoke first-error step)) when every smoke E-pin remember run failed, and as a full remember tape when any of those runs was exact or that tick budget reaches T. The eval also prices two certifications and two full pin tapes. max_seconds is ceil(1.35 times the projection plus 180), and at least 120. A projection that needs more than 24000 seconds does not fit in one container, because the function timeout is 30000 and the subprocess must stop first.
   106	
   107	If the last rung still does not fit, `r2_cost.py` exits 2, writes that fact, and launches nothing.
   108	
   109	Train jobs are spawned and settled before any eval job. The eval's `--hist-path` is `/vol/train-{model}/edit/chosen`. The weights have to be on the volume before the eval starts.
   110	
   111	## What could be wrong
   112	
   113	The verdicts are in the results section, filled from the job files. The checks are:
   114	
   115	1. **E-pin is not the certified function.** Ruled out when every E-pin table re-certifies, every E-pin pin run is trace-exact at the tape length, the bitwise-equal pin ticks equal the pin ticks, none are off-table, there is no logit mismatch, and each E-pin sha256 matches `PIN_SHA`.
   116	2. **Remember evicts or restarts positions.** Ruled out when every remember run, both edits, has `stream_tokens = 2 × ticks` and `max_delta = 0`.
   117	3. **E-hist was trained as pinned blocks.** Ruled out when each train record has 100 steps, trajectory length 256, 768 supervised positions, `causal_mismatches` 0, `presentation.ok`, and the saved example passes `presentation_ok` again at report time, and the train sha256 equals the sha256 of the E-hist file the eval loaded.
   118	
   119	## Amendments
   120

   195	## First-error anatomy
   196	
   197	One count per run. Step: the argmax is a state of the table and not the true next state. Reading: the argmax is not a state. Remember stops at that state. Pin runs the whole tape.

   235	def layer_summary(model, past):
   236	    cfg = U.core(model).config
   237	    types = list(getattr(cfg, "layer_types", []) or [])
   238	    return {"sliding_window": getattr(cfg, "sliding_window", None),
   239	            "n_sliding_layers": sum(1 for layer in past.layers if getattr(layer, "is_sliding", False)),
   240	            "n_full_layers": sum(1 for layer in past.layers if not getattr(layer, "is_sliding", False)),
   241	            "layer_types": {t: types.count(t) for t in sorted(set(types))}}

   243	
   244	def classify(nxt, s, e, delta, state_ids, event_ids, nl):
   245	    """BRIDGE / PROMPTBOX split, on a tick whose event was streamed in.
   246	    step: the argmax is a state of the table, and not δ(s, e).
   247	    reading: the argmax is not a state (an event, the newline, or some other token)."""
   248	    if nxt in state_ids:
   249	        kind, akind = "step", "state"
   250	    elif nxt in event_ids:
   251	        kind, akind = "reading", "event"
   252	    elif nxt == nl:
   253	        kind, akind = "reading", "newline"
   254	    else:
   255	        kind, akind = "reading", "other"
   256	    other = False
   257	    if akind == "state":
   258	        other = any(delta.get((s2, e)) == nxt and s2 != s for s2 in state_ids)
   259	    return kind, akind, other
   260	
   261	
   262	def run_one(model, pi, policy_code, s0, tape, want, delta, cert_logits, state_ids, event_ids, nl, stop_early):
   263	    import torch
   264	    _ROT["max_delta"] = 0
   265	    past = pb_pin.prefill(model, pi)
   266	    P = len(pi)
   267	    r = bi_stream.Run(model, past, P, policy_code)
   268	    s, got = s0, []
   269	    bitwise = off = 0
   270	    first = kind = akind = None
   271	    other = copy_state = False
   272	    t0 = time.time()
   273	    for i, e in enumerate(tape, start=1):
   274	        lg = r.tick(i, s, e)
   275	        nxt = pb_pin.top2(lg)[0]
   276	        if policy_code == "PIN":
   277	            if (s, e) not in cert_logits:
   278	                off += 1
   279	            elif torch.equal(lg.detach(), cert_logits[(s, e)]):
   280	                bitwise += 1
   281	        got.append(nxt)
   282	        if first is None and nxt != want[i - 1]:
   283	            first = i - 1
   284	            kind, akind, other = classify(nxt, s, e, delta, state_ids, event_ids, nl)
   285	            copy_state = nxt == s
   286	            if stop_early:
   287	                break
   288	        s = nxt
   289	        if i < len(tape):
   290	            r.feed_state(s)
   291	        if policy_code == "PIN" and i % 512 == 0:
   292	            print(json.dumps({"pin_tick": i, "bitwise": bitwise}), flush=True)
   293	    ticks = len(got)
   294	    if policy_code == "PIN":
   295	        if r.j != 0 or _ROT["max_delta"] != 0:
   296	            raise RuntimeError(f"pin grew the cache: j={r.j} max_delta={_ROT['max_delta']}")
   297	        if ticks != len(tape):
   298	            raise RuntimeError(f"pin stopped at {ticks}")
   299	    else:
   300	        if r.j != 2 * ticks:
   301	            raise RuntimeError(f"{policy_code} stream length j={r.j} ticks={ticks}")
   302	        if _ROT["max_delta"] != 0 and policy_code == "FULL":
   303	            raise RuntimeError("remember re-rotated keys")
   304	        if policy_code.startswith("SW") and ticks >= 4 and _ROT["max_delta"] <= 0:
   305	            raise RuntimeError("window never re-rotated")
   306	    del past, r
   307	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   308	    return {"ticks": ticks, "first_error": first, "first_error_step": None if first is None else first + 1,
   309	            "first_error_kind": kind, "argmax_kind": akind, "other_row_same_event": other, "copy_state": copy_state,
   310	            "pin_bitwise": bitwise, "pin_off_table": off,
   311	            "pin_mismatch": (ticks - bitwise - off) if policy_code == "PIN" else 0,
   312	            "stream_tokens": None if policy_code == "PIN" else 2 * ticks,
   313	            "max_delta": _ROT["max_delta"], "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 2)}
   314	
   315	
   316	def draw_selected(pool, indices):
   317	    rng = random.Random(M.TABLE_SEED)
   318	    tables = [pb_pin.draw_table(rng, pool["test"]) for _ in range(128)]
   319	    return {i: tables[i] for i in indices}

   322	def save_all(path, payload):
   323	    atomic_json(path, payload)
   324	    # the recount reads this csv; it is written from the same payload as the json
   325	    lines = ["cohort,model,table,rows,bin,shape,tape,policy,length,T,exact,first_error_step,kind,argmax_kind,"
   326	             "pin_bitwise,pin_ticks,pin_off_table,cert_pass,stream_tokens,max_delta,copy_state,other_row"]
   327	    for run in payload["runs"]:
   328	        for L, exact in run["exact_at"].items():

    45	def layout(ex):
    46	    """Python view of one example, before padding. Positions continue from 0."""
    47	    pi = list(ex["pi"])
    48	    P = len(pi)
    49	    ids = list(pi)
    50	    steer = [0] * P
    51	    lab = []
    52	    for (s, e) in ex["blocks"]:
    53	        ids.append(int(s))
    54	        ids.append(int(e))
    55	        steer.append(1)
    56	        steer.append(1)
    57	        lab.append(len(ids) - 1)
    58	    if lab != [P + 2 * i + 1 for i in range(len(ex["blocks"]))]:
    59	        raise RuntimeError("event positions are not the second token of each tick")
    60	    return {"ids": ids, "pos": list(range(len(ids))), "steer": steer, "lab": lab, "P": P,
    61	            "labels": [int(x) for x in ex["labels"]]}
    62	
    63	
    64	def history_tensors(examples):
    65	    """Pad a list of history examples. Causal visibility, steer on the stream only."""
    66	    import torch
    67	    laid = [layout(ex) for ex in examples]
    68	    L = max(len(one["ids"]) for one in laid)
    69	    B = len(laid)
    70	    ids = torch.full((B, L), 0, dtype=torch.long)
    71	    pos = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.long)
    72	    allow = torch.zeros((B, 1, L, L), dtype=torch.bool)
    73	    steer = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.bool)
    74	    rows, cols, labels = [], [], []
    75	    stats = []
    76	    for i, one in enumerate(laid):
    77	        n = len(one["ids"])
    78	        P = one["P"]
    79	        ids[i, :n] = torch.tensor(one["ids"])
    80	        pos[i, :n] = torch.tensor(one["pos"])
    81	        idx = torch.arange(n)
    82	        allow[i, 0, :n, :n] = idx[:, None] >= idx[None, :]
    83	        if n < L:
    84	            pad = torch.arange(n, L)
    85	            allow[i, 0, pad, pad] = True
    86	        steer[i, P:n] = True
    87	        for p, lab in zip(one["lab"], one["labels"]):
    88	            rows.append(i)
    89	            cols.append(p)
    90	            labels.append(lab)
    91	        stats.append({
    92	            "pi_len": P, "seq_len": n, "last_pos": int(one["pos"][-1]), "n_labels": len(one["labels"]),
    93	            "steer_on_pi": int(sum(one["steer"][:P])), "steer_on_stream": int(sum(one["steer"][P:])),
    94	            "traj_len": len(one["labels"]),
    95	        })
    96	    return (ids, pos, allow, torch.tensor(rows), torch.tensor(cols), torch.tensor(labels), steer, stats)

    98	
    99	def decimate(pairs, first_step, cap=96):
   100	    """Keep the shape of a margin trace without storing every tick."""
   101	    if not pairs:
   102	        return []
   103	    pairs = [(int(step), float(margin)) for step, margin in pairs]
   104	    if len(pairs) <= cap:
   105	        keep = list(pairs)
   106	    else:
   107	        stride = (len(pairs) + cap - 1) // cap
   108	        keep = list(pairs[::stride])
   109	        if keep[-1][0] != pairs[-1][0]:
   110	            keep.append(pairs[-1])
   111	        if keep[0][0] != pairs[0][0]:
   112	            keep.insert(0, pairs[0])
   113	    if first_step is not None:
   114	        hit = next((pair for pair in pairs if pair[0] == int(first_step)), None)
   115	        if hit is not None and all(pair[0] != hit[0] for pair in keep):
   116	            keep.append(hit)
   117	    ordered = sorted({pair[0]: pair for pair in keep}.values())
   118	    return [[step, round(margin, 4)] for step, margin in ordered]

   121	def presentation_ok(stat, traj_len):
   122	    """True when this example is a continuing causal trajectory of `traj_len` events."""
   123	    return (stat["n_labels"] == traj_len
   124	            and stat["traj_len"] == traj_len
   125	            and stat["last_pos"] == stat["seq_len"] - 1
   126	            and stat["last_pos"] == stat["pi_len"] + 2 * traj_len - 1
   127	            and stat["steer_on_pi"] == 0
   128	            and stat["steer_on_stream"] == 2 * traj_len)

   103	def draw_table(rng, names, n=None, a=None, shape=None):
   104	    """A total table over n states and a events (n·a ≤ 256 rows). Shapes: random destinations, a permutation per
   105	    event (swaps, moves), a bounded counter (±1 or hold), mostly self-loops (filters, flags). Rows are written
   106	    state-major (as a person writes them) or shuffled, half each."""
   107	    n = n or rng.randint(2, 32)
   108	    a = a or rng.randint(2, 8)
   109	    shape = shape or rng.choices(["random", "perm", "counter", "sticky"], weights=[0.55, 0.2, 0.1, 0.15])[0]
   110	    picked = rng.sample(list(names), n + a)
   111	    states, events = picked[:n], picked[n:]
   112	    delta = {}
   113	    for j, e in enumerate(events):
   114	        if shape == "perm":
   115	            perm = states[:]
   116	            rng.shuffle(perm)
   117	            for s, t in zip(states, perm):
   118	                delta[(s, e)] = t
   119	        elif shape == "counter":
   120	            step = rng.choice([-1, 0, 1])
   121	            for i, s in enumerate(states):
   122	                delta[(s, e)] = states[min(n - 1, max(0, i + step))]
   123	        else:
   124	            for s in states:
   125	                stay = shape == "sticky" and rng.random() < 0.8
   126	                delta[(s, e)] = s if stay else rng.choice(states)
   127	    rows = [(s, e, delta[(s, e)]) for s in states for e in events]
   128	    if rng.random() < 0.5:
   129	        rng.shuffle(rows)
   130	    return {"states": states, "events": events, "rows": rows, "shape": shape, "start": states[0]}

   137	def encode_table(table, id_of, nl):
   138	    ids = []
   139	    for s, e, t in table["rows"]:
   140	        ids += [id_of[s], id_of[e], id_of[t], nl]
   141	    return ids

   145	    return "4-16" if n_rows <= 16 else "17-64" if n_rows <= 64 else "65-128" if n_rows <= 128 else "129-256"

   148	def pinned_example(rng, pool, split="train", blocks=16):
   149	    """One packed training sequence: Π, then `blocks` (s, e) blocks, each labelled with δ(s, e)."""
   150	    table = draw_table(rng, pool[split])
   151	    pi = encode_table(table, pool["id_of"], pool["nl"])
   152	    pairs = [(s, e, t) for s, e, t in table["rows"]]
   153	    pick = rng.sample(pairs, min(blocks, len(pairs)))
   154	    while len(pick) < blocks:
   155	        pick.append(rng.choice(pairs))
   156	    io = pool["id_of"]
   157	    return {"pi": pi, "blocks": [(io[s], io[e]) for s, e, _ in pick], "labels": [io[t] for _, _, t in pick]}
   158	
   159	
   160	def packed(ex):
   161	    """Token ids, position ids, owner per position (-1 on Π, block index otherwise) and label positions."""
   162	    P = len(ex["pi"])
   163	    ids, pos, owner = list(ex["pi"]), list(range(P)), [-1] * P
   164	    lab_pos = []
   165	    for b, (s, e) in enumerate(ex["blocks"]):
   166	        ids += [s, e]
   167	        pos += [P, P + 1]
   168	        owner += [b, b]
   169	        lab_pos.append(len(ids) - 1)
   170	    return ids, pos, owner, lab_pos
   171	
   172	
   173	def packed_batch(exs, pad_id):
   174	    """Tensors for a micro-batch of packed examples: ids, positions, a boolean (B, 1, L, L) visibility mask
   175	    (True = may attend), and (row, column, label) triples for the loss."""
   176	    import torch
   177	    seqs = [packed(ex) for ex in exs]
   178	    L = max(len(s[0]) for s in seqs)
   179	    B = len(seqs)
   180	    ids = torch.full((B, L), pad_id, dtype=torch.long)
   181	    pos = torch.zeros((B, L), dtype=torch.long)
   182	    allow = torch.zeros((B, 1, L, L), dtype=torch.bool)
   183	    rows, cols, labels = [], [], []
   184	    for i, ((sid, spos, owner, lab_pos), ex) in enumerate(zip(seqs, exs)):
   185	        n = len(sid)
   186	        ids[i, :n] = torch.tensor(sid)
   187	        pos[i, :n] = torch.tensor(spos)
   188	        own = torch.tensor(owner)
   189	        P = int((own < 0).sum())
   190	        q = torch.arange(n)
   191	        causal = q[:, None] >= q[None, :]
   192	        same = own[:, None] == own[None, :]
   193	        to_pi = (own[None, :] < 0).expand(n, n)
   194	        allow[i, 0, :n, :n] = causal & (same | to_pi)
   195	        allow[i, 0, :P, P:n] = False
   196	        if n < L:
   197	            idx = torch.arange(n, L)
   198	            allow[i, 0, idx, idx] = True
   199	            pos[i, n:] = 0
   200	        for p, lab in zip(lab_pos, ex["labels"]):
   201	            rows.append(i)
   202	            cols.append(p)
   203	            labels.append(lab)
   204	    return ids, pos, allow, torch.tensor(rows), torch.tensor(cols), torch.tensor(labels)
   209	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   210	    import torch
   211	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=model.device)
   212	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=model.device)[None]
   213	    with torch.no_grad():
   214	        return model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values

   217	def _clone(past):
   218	    import copy
   219	    import torch
   220	    if hasattr(past, "layers"):
   221	        new = copy.copy(past)
   222	        new.layers = []
   223	        for layer in past.layers:
   224	            c = copy.copy(layer)
   225	            for attr in ("keys", "values"):
   226	                v = getattr(c, attr, None)
   227	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   228	                    setattr(c, attr, v.clone())
   229	            new.layers.append(c)
   230	        return new
   231	    if hasattr(past, "key_cache"):
   232	        new = copy.copy(past)
   233	        new.key_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.key_cache]
   234	        new.value_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.value_cache]
   235	        return new
   236	    return tuple(tuple(t.clone() for t in layer) for layer in past)
   237	
   238	
   239	def tick(model, past, P, s_id, e_id):
   240	    """One tick: the block [s][e] at positions P, P+1 over a copy of Π's cache. Returns the full-vocabulary
   241	    logits at the event position (the copy is discarded, so Π's cache is never written)."""
   242	    import torch
   243	    pos = torch.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=model.device)
   244	    with torch.no_grad():
   245	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[s_id, e_id]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos,
   246	                    cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=_clone(past), use_cache=True)
   247	    return out.logits[0, -1]
   248	
   249	
   250	def top2(logits):
   251	    import torch
   252	    v, i = torch.topk(logits.float(), 2)
   253	    return int(i[0]), float(v[0] - v[1])
   254	
   255	
   256	def certify(model, pi_ids, rows, keep_logits=False):
   257	    """Every row of S × B, one batch-1 tick each over the same cached Π. Passes iff every argmax (full
   258	    vocabulary) is the row's destination id with a strictly positive margin."""
   259	    past = prefill(model, pi_ids)
   260	    P = len(pi_ids)
   261	    out, logits = [], {}
   262	    for s, e, t in rows:
   263	        lg = tick(model, past, P, s, e)
   264	        arg, margin = top2(lg)
   265	        out.append({"s": s, "e": e, "t": t, "argmax": arg, "margin": round(margin, 4),
   266	                    "ok": arg == t and margin > 0})
   267	        if keep_logits:
   268	            logits[(s, e)] = lg.clone()
   269	    passed = all(r["ok"] for r in out)
   270	    return {"pass": passed, "rows": out, "min_margin": min(r["margin"] for r in out) if out else None,
   271	            "rows_wrong": sum(not r["ok"] for r in out)}, past, logits

    47	def kv_of(past):
    48	    return [(layer.keys, layer.values) for layer in past.layers]
    49	
    50	
    51	def cache_from(template, kvs):
    52	    new = copy.copy(template)
    53	    new.layers = []
    54	    for layer, (k, v) in zip(template.layers, kvs):
    55	        c = copy.copy(layer)
    56	        c.keys, c.values = k, v
    57	        new.layers.append(c)
    58	    return new
    59	
    60	
    61	def forward(model, template, kvs, tokens, positions):
    62	    """One forward of `tokens` at RoPE `positions` over a cache holding exactly `kvs` (per layer). The mask is causal
    63	    over (cache, new tokens): each new token sees the whole cache. Returns (last logits, new tokens' per-layer kv)."""
    64	    import torch
    65	    cache = cache_from(template, kvs)
    66	    q = len(tokens)
    67	    with torch.no_grad():
    68	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([tokens], device=model.device),
    69	                    position_ids=torch.tensor([positions], device=model.device), past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
    70	    # clone: a view would keep the whole concatenated tensor of every layer alive
    71	    new = [(layer.keys[..., -q:, :].clone(), layer.values[..., -q:, :].clone()) for layer in cache.layers]
    72	    return out.logits[0, -1], new

   119	class Run:
   120	    """Free-running stream under one policy. Returns per-tick logits through `on_tick`."""
   121	
   122	    def __init__(self, model, past_pi, P, policy):
   123	        self.model, self.template, self.P, self.policy = model, past_pi, P, policy
   124	        self.pi = kv_of(past_pi)
   125	        self.window = []  # per-token kv of the stream tokens kept (SW, FULL)
   126	        self.j = 0        # stream index of the next token (s0 is index 0)
   127	        self.cache = cache_from(past_pi, self.pi)  # persistent cache for KV2, ADV, TOK: grows, then is evicted
   128	
   129	    def _token(self, tok, k):
   130	        """Process one stream token under a uniform window k (None = FULL) at its text position."""
   131	        pos = self.P + self.j
   132	        if k is None:
   133	            delta, keep = 0, self.window
   134	        else:
   135	            keep = self.window[-(k - 1):] if k > 1 else []
   136	            first = self.j - len(keep)               # stream index of the oldest token in view
   137	            delta = first if self.policy.endswith("b") else 0
   138	        pi = rotate_kv(self.model, self.pi, delta)
   139	        kvs = cat_kv(pi, *keep) if keep else pi
   140	        lg, new = forward(self.model, self.template, kvs, [tok], [pos])
   141	        self.window.append(new)
   142	        if k is not None and len(self.window) > max(k - 1, 0):
   143	            self.window = self.window[-(k - 1):] if k > 1 else []
   144	        if k is None:
   145	            # FULL keeps everything; store as one growing kv to keep cat cheap
   146	            if len(self.window) > 64:
   147	                self.window = [cat_kv(*self.window)]
   148	        self.j += 1
   149	        return lg
   150	
   151	    def tick(self, i, s, e):
   152	        """Tick i (1-based): the block [s][e]; returns the logits at e's position."""
   153	        p, P = self.policy, self.P
   154	        if p == "PIN":
   155	            return pb_pin.tick(self.model, self.template, P, s, e)
   156	        if p in ("KV2", "ADV"):
   157	            base = P if p == "KV2" else P + 2 * (i - 1)
   158	            lg = step(self.model, self.cache, [s, e], [base, base + 1])
   159	            evict_to(self.cache, P)
   160	            return lg
   161	        if p == "TOK":
   162	            step(self.model, self.cache, [s], [P])
   163	            lg = step(self.model, self.cache, [e], [P + 1])
   164	            evict_to(self.cache, P)
   165	            return lg
   166	        if p.startswith("SW") or p == "FULL":
   167	            k = None if p == "FULL" else int(p[2])
   168	            if i == 1:
   169	                self._token(s, k)          # s0 enters the stream
   170	            return self._token(e, k)       # s_{i-1} (i > 1) was fed right after the previous tick
   171	        raise ValueError(p)
   172	
   173	    def feed_state(self, s):
   174	        """Decoding feeds the emitted state back as the next stream token (SW and FULL only)."""
   175	        if self.policy.startswith("SW") or self.policy == "FULL":
   176	            k = None if self.policy == "FULL" else int(self.policy[2])
   177	            self._token(s, k)
   145	def name_pool(tok, n_train=240, n_test=120):
   146	    """pb_pin.name_pool with the space marker and the encoding generalised (module docstring)."""
   147	    enc = Enc(tok)
   148	    nl = enc.ids("\n")
   149	    assert len(nl) == 1, "newline is not one token"
   150	    nl = nl[0]
   151	    cands = _candidates(enc)
   152	    cut = int(0.66 * len(cands))
   153	    id_of, pools = {}, {}
   154	    for split, src, n in (("train", cands[:cut], n_train), ("test", cands[cut:], n_test)):
   155	        chosen = []
   156	        for w, _kind in src:
   157	            if len(chosen) >= n:
   158	                break
   159	            wid = enc.ids(" " + w)[0]
   160	            if w in id_of or wid in id_of.values():
   161	                continue
   162	            id_of[w] = wid
   163	            if _fits(enc, w, wid, [c for c in id_of if c != w], id_of, nl):
   164	                chosen.append(w)
   165	            else:
   166	                del id_of[w]
   167	        pools[split] = chosen
   168	    for name, text in ([(d, d) for d in "0123456789"] + [(c, " " + c) for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"]
   169	                       + [(b, b) for b in "()[]"]):
   170	        ids = enc.ids(text)
   171	        if len(ids) == 1 and ids[0] not in id_of.values() and name not in id_of:
   172	            id_of[name] = ids[0]
   173	            pools["train"].append(name)
   174	    kinds = dict(cands)
   175	    assert len(pools["test"]) == n_test, f"held-out pool has {len(pools['test'])} names, not {n_test}"
   176	    return {"train": pools["train"], "test": pools["test"], "id_of": id_of, "nl": nl,
   177	            "marker": enc.marker, "direct": enc.direct, "candidates": len(cands),
   178	            "kinds": {s: {k: sum(kinds.get(w) == k for w in pools[s]) for k in ("lower", "cap", "code", "letter")}
   179	                      | {"extra": sum(w not in kinds for w in pools[s])} for s in ("train", "test")}}

   220	def core(model):
   221	    """The underlying *ForCausalLM (through a PEFT wrapper if there is one)."""
   222	    try:
   223	        from peft import PeftModel
   224	        if isinstance(model, PeftModel):
   225	            return model.base_model.model
   226	    except ImportError:
   227	        pass
   228	    return model

   230	
   231	def head_logits(c, h):
   232	    """The model's output logits from final hidden states (lm_head, then Gemma-2's final soft cap if it has one).
   233	    Returned in float32 for the loss, as bi_train's ce_sum."""
   234	    lg = c.lm_head(h)
   235	    cap = getattr(c.config, "final_logit_softcapping", None)
   236	    if cap:
   237	        lg = (lg / cap).tanh() * cap
   238	    return lg.float()

   241	def model_mask(c, allow):
   242	    """A boolean 4D visibility mask (True = may attend) in the form this model's attention adds or reads: as is for
   243	    sdpa, additive (0 / most negative) for eager, which adds the 4D mask to its scores."""
   244	    import torch
   245	    if c.config._attn_implementation == "eager":
   246	        dt = c.model.embed_tokens.weight.dtype
   247	        return torch.zeros(allow.shape, dtype=dt, device=allow.device).masked_fill(~allow, torch.finfo(dt).min)
   248	    return allow

   251	def count_model_params(model):
   252	    return int(sum(p.numel() for p in core(model).parameters()))

   255	class Steer:
   256	    """bi_edit.Steer, unchanged: adds `vec` to decoder layer `layer`'s output. where='block': only while `on`
   257	    (off during Π's prefill), and only at positions where `mask` is True if a mask is set."""
   258	
   259	    def __init__(self, model, layer, where, vec=None):
   260	        import torch
   261	        c = core(model)
   262	        d = c.config.hidden_size
   263	        self.where, self.layer = where, layer
   264	        self.vec = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(d, dtype=torch.float32, device=c.lm_head.weight.device)
   265	                                      if vec is None else vec.float().to(c.lm_head.weight.device))
   266	        self.on, self.mask = True, None
   267	        self.handle = c.model.layers[layer].register_forward_hook(self._hook)
   268	
   269	    def _hook(self, module, inp, out):
   270	        if self.where == "block" and not self.on:
   271	            return out
   272	        h = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
   273	        add = self.vec.to(h.dtype)
   274	        if self.where == "block" and self.mask is not None:
   275	            h = h + self.mask[..., None].to(h.dtype) * add
   276	        else:
   277	            h = h + add
   278	        return (h,) + tuple(out[1:]) if isinstance(out, tuple) else h
   279	
   280	    def save(self, path):
   281	        import torch
   282	        path = pathlib.Path(path)
   283	        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   284	        torch.save({"layer": self.layer, "where": self.where, "vec": self.vec.detach().cpu()}, path / "steer.pt")
   285	
   286	    def remove(self):
   287	        self.handle.remove()
   288	
   289	
   290	def block_only_prefill(steer):
   291	    """bi_edit.block_only_prefill: with a block-only vector, Π's prefill runs without it (wraps pb_pin.prefill)."""
   292	    if steer is None or steer.where != "block":
   293	        return lambda: None
   294	    orig = pb_pin.prefill
   295	
   296	    def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   297	        steer.on = False
   298	        try:
   299	            return orig(model, pi_ids)
   300	        finally:
   301	            steer.on = True
   302	    pb_pin.prefill = prefill
   303	
   304	    def undo():
   305	        pb_pin.prefill = orig
   306	    return undo

    40	def draw_selected(pool, indices):
    41	    rng = random.Random(M.TABLE_SEED)
    42	    tables = [pb_pin.draw_table(rng, pool["test"]) for _ in range(128)]
    43	    return {i: tables[i] for i in indices}

    46	def run_tape(model, pi, policy, s0, tape, want, delta, cert_logits, state_ids, event_ids, nl, stop_early,
    47	             mark_every=0):
    48	    """One policy on one tape. `tape` items are event token ids. Steps are 1-based."""
    49	    import torch
    50	    rem_job._ROT["max_delta"] = 0
    51	    past = pb_pin.prefill(model, pi)
    52	    P = len(pi)
    53	    runner = rem_job.bi_stream.Run(model, past, P, policy)
    54	    s = s0
    55	    margins = []
    56	    marks = []
    57	    bitwise = off = 0
    58	    first = kind = akind = None
    59	    other = copy_state = False
    60	    t0 = time.time()
    61	    ticks = 0
    62	    for i, event in enumerate(tape, start=1):
    63	        lg = runner.tick(i, s, event)
    64	        nxt, margin = pb_pin.top2(lg)
    65	        margins.append((i, margin))
    66	        if policy == "PIN" and cert_logits is not None:
    67	            if (s, event) not in cert_logits:
    68	                off += 1
    69	            elif torch.equal(lg.detach(), cert_logits[(s, event)]):
    70	                bitwise += 1
    71	        if first is None and nxt != want[i - 1]:
    72	            first = i - 1
    73	            kind, akind, other = rem_job.classify(nxt, s, event, delta, state_ids, event_ids, nl)
    74	            copy_state = nxt == s
    75	            if stop_early:
    76	                ticks = i
    77	                break
    78	        s = nxt
    79	        ticks = i
    80	        if i < len(tape):
    81	            runner.feed_state(s)
    82	        if mark_every and i % mark_every == 0:
    83	            marks.append({"ticks": i, "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 4)})
    84	        if policy == "PIN" and i % 512 == 0:
    85	            log(pin_tick=i, bitwise=bitwise)
    86	    if mark_every and (not marks or marks[-1]["ticks"] != ticks):
    87	        marks.append({"ticks": ticks, "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 4)})
    88	    if policy == "PIN":
    89	        if runner.j != 0 or rem_job._ROT["max_delta"] != 0:
    90	            raise RuntimeError(f"pin grew the cache: j={runner.j} max_delta={rem_job._ROT['max_delta']}")
    91	        if ticks != len(tape):
    92	            raise RuntimeError(f"pin stopped at {ticks}")
    93	    else:
    94	        if runner.j != 2 * ticks:
    95	            raise RuntimeError(f"{policy} stream length j={runner.j} ticks={ticks}")
    96	        if rem_job._ROT["max_delta"] != 0 and policy == "FULL":
    97	            raise RuntimeError("remember re-rotated keys")
    98	    del past, runner
    99	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   100	    first_step = None if first is None else first + 1
   101	    return {
   102	        "ticks": ticks, "first_error": first, "first_error_step": first_step, "first_error_kind": kind,
   103	        "argmax_kind": akind, "other_row_same_event": other, "copy_state": copy_state,
   104	        "pin_bitwise": bitwise, "pin_off_table": off,
   105	        "pin_mismatch": (ticks - bitwise - off) if policy == "PIN" else 0,
   106	        "stream_tokens": None if policy == "PIN" else 2 * ticks,
   107	        "max_delta": rem_job._ROT["max_delta"], "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 4),
   108	        "margin_min": None if not margins else round(min(m for _s, m in margins), 4),
   109	        "margin_trace": r2_data.decimate(margins, first_step),
   110	        "margin_at_step": {step: round(margin, 4) for step, margin in margins},
   111	        "pi_len": P, "marks": marks,
   112	    }

   115	def save_all(path, payload):
   116	    atomic_json(path, payload)
   117	    lines = []
   118	    for run in payload["runs"]:
   119	        for length, exact in sorted(run["exact_at"].items(), key=lambda kv: int(kv[0])):
   120	            full = run["policy"] == "pin" and int(length) == int(run["T"])
   121	            margin_at = run["margin_at"].get(str(length))
   122	            lines.append({
   123	                "model": payload["model"], "edit": run["edit"], "table": run["table"], "rows": run["rows"],
   124	                "bin": run["bin"], "shape": run["shape"], "tape": run["tape"], "policy": run["policy"],
   125	                "length": length, "T": run["T"], "exact": int(bool(exact)),
   126	                "first_error_step": "" if run["first_error_step"] is None else run["first_error_step"],
   127	                "kind": run["first_error_kind"] or "", "argmax_kind": run["argmax_kind"] or "",
   128	                "pin_bitwise": run["pin_bitwise"] if full else "", "pin_ticks": run["ticks"] if full else "",
   129	                "pin_off_table": run["pin_off_table"] if full else "", "cert_pass": int(bool(run["cert_pass"])),
   130	                "stream_tokens": "" if run["stream_tokens"] is None else run["stream_tokens"],
   131	                "max_delta": run["max_delta"], "copy_state": int(bool(run["copy_state"])),
   132	                "other_row": int(bool(run["other_row_same_event"])),
   133	                "margin_min": "" if run["margin_min"] is None else run["margin_min"],
   134	                "margin_at": "" if margin_at is None else margin_at,
   135	            })
   136	    csv_path = path.with_name("runs.csv")
   137	    fields = ["model", "edit", "table", "rows", "bin", "shape", "tape", "policy", "length", "T", "exact",
   138	              "first_error_step", "kind", "argmax_kind", "pin_bitwise", "pin_ticks", "pin_off_table", "cert_pass",
   139	              "stream_tokens", "max_delta", "copy_state", "other_row", "margin_min", "margin_at"]
   140	    with csv_path.open("w", newline="") as f:
   141	        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fields)
   142	        writer.writeheader()
   143	        writer.writerows(lines)
   144	
   145	
   146	def _finished(run, T):
   147	    if int(run.get("T", 0)) < T:
   148	        return False
   149	    if run.get("first_error") is None and int(run.get("ticks", 0)) < T:
   150	        return False
   151	    return True
   152

   154	def _key(run):
   155	    return run["edit"], int(run["table"]), int(run["tape"]), run["policy"]

   158	def evaluate(name, edits, indices, lengths, tapes, seed, hist_path, out, probe_ticks=0, carry=None):
   188	    import transformers
   189	    payload = {"model": name, "edits": edits, "snapshot": None, "lengths": lengths, "tapes": tapes, "T": T,
   190	               "seed": seed, "table_seed": M.TABLE_SEED, "policies": list(M.POLICIES), "host": host,
   191	               "carry_host": carry_host, "torch": torch.__version__, "transformers": transformers.__version__,
   192	               "at": stamp(), "runs": list(carried), "tables": [], "probe": None, "complete": False}
   193	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   194	    path = out / "runs.json"
   195	    save_all(path, payload)
   196	    layers = None
   197	    for edit_name in edits:
   198	        t_load = time.time()
   199	        model, snapshot = r2_load.load_base(name)
   200	        payload["snapshot"] = snapshot
   201	        if edit_name == "e-pin":
   202	            model, loaded, undo, meta = r2_load.attach_pin(model, name)
   203	        else:
   204	            model, loaded, undo, meta = r2_load.attach(model, M.kind(name), hist_path)
   205	        meta = dict(meta, edit=edit_name)
   206	        rope = rem_job.rope_check(model)
   207	        log(edit=edit_name, sha256=meta["sha256"], load_s=round(time.time() - t_load, 1), rope=rope)
   208	        if layers is None:
   209	            probe_i = indices[0]
   210	            probe_pi = list(prefix) + pb_pin.encode_table(tables[probe_i], io, nl)
   211	            probe_past = pb_pin.prefill(model, probe_pi)
   212	            layers = rem_job.layer_summary(model, probe_past)
   213	            del probe_past
   214	            payload["layers"] = layers
   215	            payload["rope_check"] = rope
   216	        payload.setdefault("edit_meta", {})[edit_name] = meta
   217	        try:
   218	            for i in indices:
   219	                tb = tables[i]
   220	                fp = fingers[i]
   221	                if (len(tb["rows"]), len(tb["states"]), len(tb["events"]), tb["shape"]) != (fp[1], fp[2], fp[3], fp[4]):
   222	                    raise RuntimeError(f"table {i} does not match the registry {fp}")
   223	                pi = list(prefix) + pb_pin.encode_table(tb, io, nl)
   224	                rows = [(io[s_], io[e_], io[t_]) for s_, e_, t_ in tb["rows"]]
   225	                cert, past, logits = pb_pin.certify(model, pi, rows, keep_logits=True)
   226	                del past
   227	                logits = {k: v.detach() for k, v in logits.items()}
   228	                table_rec = {"i": i, "edit": edit_name, "rows": fp[1], "n": fp[2], "a": fp[3], "shape": fp[4],
   229	                             "bin": fp[5], "expect_pass": fp[6], "cert_pass": cert["pass"],
   230	                             "rows_wrong": cert["rows_wrong"], "min_margin": cert["min_margin"]}
   231	                payload["tables"] = [t for t in payload["tables"] if not (t["edit"] == edit_name and t["i"] == i)]
   232	                payload["tables"].append(table_rec)
   233	                save_all(path, payload)
   234	                log(**table_rec)
   235	                if edit_name == "e-pin" and not cert["pass"]:
   236	                    raise RuntimeError(f"E-pin table {i} failed re-certification ({cert['rows_wrong']} rows)")
   237	                delta = {(io[s_], io[e_]): io[t_] for s_, e_, t_ in tb["rows"]}
   238	                state_ids = {io[s_] for s_ in tb["states"]}
   239	                event_ids = {io[e_] for e_ in tb["events"]}
   240	                s0 = io[tb["start"]]
   241	                drawn = M.event_tapes(len(tb["events"]), i, n=tapes, T=T, seed=seed)
   242	                for tape_k, idxs in enumerate(drawn):
   243	                    tape = [io[tb["events"][j]] for j in idxs]
   244	                    want, s_true = [], s0
   245	                    for event in tape:
   246	                        s_true = delta[(s_true, event)]
   247	                        want.append(s_true)
   248	                    for policy in M.POLICIES:
   249	                        if (edit_name, i, tape_k, policy) in done:
   250	                            log(skip=True, edit=edit_name, table=i, tape=tape_k, policy=policy)
   251	                            continue
   252	                        code = {"pin": "PIN", "remember": "FULL"}[policy]
   253	                        rec = run_tape(model, pi, code, s0, tape, want, delta, logits, state_ids, event_ids, nl,
   254	                                       stop_early=(policy != "pin"))
   255	                        if policy != "pin" and rec["first_error"] is not None and rec["ticks"] != rec["first_error"] + 1:
   256	                            raise RuntimeError("remember did not stop on the failing tick")
   257	                        if rec["first_error"] is None and rec["ticks"] != T:
   258	                            raise RuntimeError("exact run did not finish the tape")
   259	                        margin_at = {str(L): rec["margin_at_step"].get(L) for L in lengths}
   260	                        row = {"edit": edit_name, "table": i, "rows": fp[1], "bin": fp[5], "shape": fp[4],
   261	                               "tape": tape_k, "policy": policy, "T": T, "cert_pass": cert["pass"],
   262	                               "exact_at": {str(L): M.exact_at(rec["first_error"], L) for L in lengths},
   263	                               "margin_at": margin_at, "host": host,
   264	                               **{k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("margin_at_step", "marks")}}
   265	                        payload["runs"].append(row)
   266	                        done.add((edit_name, i, tape_k, policy))
   267	                        log(edit=edit_name, table=i, tape=tape_k, policy=policy, exact_at=row["exact_at"],
   268	                            first_error_step=row["first_error_step"], kind=row["first_error_kind"],
   269	                            ticks=row["ticks"], seconds=row["seconds"], margin_min=row["margin_min"])
   270	                        save_all(path, payload)
   271	                    if probe_ticks and edit_name == "e-pin" and i == indices[0] and payload["probe"] is None:
   272	                        probe_idxs = M.event_tapes(len(tb["events"]), i, n=1, T=probe_ticks, seed=seed)[0]
   273	                        probe_tape = [io[tb["events"][j]] for j in probe_idxs]
   274	                        probe_want, s_true = [], s0
   275	                        for event in probe_tape:
   276	                            s_true = delta[(s_true, event)]
   277	                            probe_want.append(s_true)
   278	                        pin_rec = run_tape(model, pi, "PIN", s0, probe_tape, probe_want, delta, logits, state_ids,
   279	                                           event_ids, nl, stop_early=False)
   280	                        rem_rec = run_tape(model, pi, "FULL", s0, probe_tape, probe_want, delta, logits, state_ids,
   281	                                           event_ids, nl, stop_early=False, mark_every=32)
   282	                        payload["probe"] = {"table": i, "rows": fp[1], "ticks": probe_ticks,
   283	                                            "pin_seconds": pin_rec["seconds"], "pin_ticks": pin_rec["ticks"],
   284	                                            "remember_marks": rem_rec["marks"],
   285	                                            "remember_seconds": rem_rec["seconds"],
   286	                                            "remember_ticks": rem_rec["ticks"]}
   287	                        log(probe=payload["probe"])
   288	                        save_all(path, payload)
   289	                del logits
   290	                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   291	        finally:
   292	            kind = meta["kind"]
   293	            r2_load.release(model, loaded, undo, kind)
   294	            del model, loaded, undo
   295	    payload["complete"] = True
   296	    payload["at"] = stamp()
   297	    save_all(path, payload)
   298	    log(complete=True, runs=len(payload["runs"]))
   299	    return payload

   302	def main():
   303	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   304	    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
   305	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   306	    ap.add_argument("--hist-path", default="")
   307	    ap.add_argument("--edits", default="e-pin,e-hist")
   308	    ap.add_argument("--tables", default="")
   309	    ap.add_argument("--lengths", default="256,1024,4096,8192")
   310	    ap.add_argument("--tapes", type=int, default=M.TAPES)
   311	    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=M.SEED)
   312	    ap.add_argument("--carry", default="")
   313	    a = ap.parse_args()
   314	    if a.model not in M.MODELS:
   315	        raise SystemExit(f"{a.model} is not one of the four")
   316	    if a.seed != M.SEED:
   317	        raise SystemExit("seed is not the registered seed")
   318	    if a.tapes not in (1, 2, 4):
   220	
   221	
   222	def threats_from(payloads, trains):
   223	    """The three checks. Numbers are counts over the payloads, not constants."""
   224	    e_pin_tables = [t for _j, p in payloads for t in p.get("tables", []) if t.get("edit") == "e-pin"]
   225	    e_pin_pin = [r for _j, p in payloads for r in p["runs"] if r["edit"] == "e-pin" and r["policy"] == "pin"]
   226	    remember = [r for _j, p in payloads for r in p["runs"] if r["policy"] == "remember"]
   227	    cert_fail = sum(1 for t in e_pin_tables if not t["cert_pass"])
   228	    pin_exact = sum(1 for r in e_pin_pin if r["exact_at"].get(str(r["T"])))
   229	    bitwise = sum(int(r["pin_bitwise"]) for r in e_pin_pin)
   230	    ticks = sum(int(r["ticks"]) for r in e_pin_pin)
   231	    off = sum(int(r["pin_off_table"]) for r in e_pin_pin)
   232	    mismatch = sum(int(r["pin_mismatch"]) for r in e_pin_pin)
   233	    sha_ok = 0
   234	    for _j, p in payloads:
   235	        meta = (p.get("edit_meta") or {}).get("e-pin") or {}
   236	        if meta.get("sha256") == M.PIN_SHA.get(p["model"]):
   237	            sha_ok += 1
   238	    t1 = (cert_fail == 0 and e_pin_pin and pin_exact == len(e_pin_pin) and bitwise == ticks and ticks > 0
   239	          and off == 0 and mismatch == 0 and sha_ok == len(payloads) and len(payloads) == len(M.MODELS))
   240	    d1 = (f"E-pin tables that failed re-certification: {cert_fail}. "
   241	          f"E-pin pin trace-exact runs at the tape length: {pin_exact} of {len(e_pin_pin)}. "
   242	          f"E-pin pin ticks bitwise equal to the certified row: {bitwise} of {ticks}. "
   243	          f"Off-table pin ticks: {off}. Pin logit mismatches: {mismatch}. "
   244	          f"E-pin sha256 matches the registered file: {sha_ok} of {len(payloads)}.")
   245	    stream_ok = sum(1 for r in remember if r.get("stream_tokens") == 2 * r["ticks"] and r.get("max_delta") == 0)
   246	    t2 = bool(remember) and stream_ok == len(remember)
   247	    d2 = (f"Remember runs: {len(remember)}. "
   248	          f"Runs with stream_tokens = 2 × ticks and max_delta = 0: {stream_ok}.")
   249	    pres_ok = 0
   250	    details = []
   251	    for name in M.MODELS:
   252	        rec = trains.get(name)
   253	        if not rec:
   254	            details.append(f"{name} has no train.json")
   255	            continue
   256	        chosen = rec["runs"][rec["chosen"]]
   257	        pres = chosen.get("presentation") or {}
   258	        stat = pres.get("first_batch") or {}
   259	        eval_sha = None
   260	        for _j, p in payloads:
   261	            if p["model"] == name:
   262	                eval_sha = ((p.get("edit_meta") or {}).get("e-hist") or {}).get("sha256")
   263	        good = (pres.get("ok") and pres.get("causal_mismatches") == 0 and stat
   264	                and pres.get("steps_checked") == M.STEPS
   265	                and pres.get("supervised_per_step") == M.SUPERVISED
   266	                and r2_data.presentation_ok(stat, rec["traj_len"])
   267	                and rec["supervised_per_step"] == M.SUPERVISED
   268	                and rec["n_traj"] * rec["traj_len"] == M.SUPERVISED
   269	                and rec["steps"] == M.STEPS and rec["traj_len"] == M.TRAJ_LEN
   270	                and rec.get("sha256") == eval_sha and eval_sha)
   271	        pres_ok += int(bool(good))
   272	        details.append(
   273	            f"{name} steps {rec.get('steps')} traj_len {rec.get('traj_len')} "
   274	            f"supervised_per_step {rec.get('supervised_per_step')} "
   275	            f"causal_mismatches {pres.get('causal_mismatches')} "
   276	            f"last_pos {stat.get('last_pos')} pi_len {stat.get('pi_len')} "
   277	            f"sha_match {rec.get('sha256') == eval_sha}")
   278	    t3 = pres_ok == len(M.MODELS)
   279	    d3 = f"Trains whose saved batch is a continuing length-{M.TRAJ_LEN} history and whose sha matches the eval load: {pres_ok} of {len(M.MODELS)}. " + " ".join(details)
   280	    return [{"id": 1, "name": "E-pin is not the certified function.", "ruled_out": t1, "detail": d1},
   281	            {"id": 2, "name": "Remember evicts or restarts positions.", "ruled_out": t2, "detail": d2},
   282	            {"id": 3, "name": "E-hist was trained as pinned blocks.", "ruled_out": t3, "detail": d3}]
   283	
   284	
   285	def _fmt(v):
   286	    return "undefined" if v is None else str(v)
   287	
   288	
   289	def _host(h):
   290	    parts = [str(h.get("gpu")), str(h.get("cpu")), f"avx512f={h.get('avx512f')}"]
   291	    if h.get("gpu_actual"):
   292	        parts.append(f"ledger gpu {h['gpu_actual']}")
   293	    if h.get("cpu_actual"):
   294	        parts.append(f"ledger cpu {h['cpu_actual']}")
   295	    return "; ".join(parts).replace("|", "/")
   296	
   297	
   298	def summary_md(s):
   299	    k = s["keys"]
   300	    lines = [
     1	"""
     2	Draft figure for REMEMBER, from results/summary.json (written by rem_report.py).
     3	
     4	White background. Cobalt is pin, rose is remember, grey is window. Matplotlib only.
     5	
     6	    python research/remember/rem_fig.py
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import json
    12	import pathlib
    13	
    14	import matplotlib
    15	
    16	matplotlib.use("Agg")
    17	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402
    18	
    19	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    20	COBALT, ROSE, GREY = "#2557A7", "#D6336C", "#6B7280"
    21	INK = "#1F2A44"
    22	STYLE = {"font.family": "sans-serif", "font.sans-serif": ["Helvetica Neue", "Helvetica", "Arial", "DejaVu Sans"],
    23	         "font.size": 7, "axes.labelsize": 7, "xtick.labelsize": 6, "ytick.labelsize": 6,
    24	         "axes.edgecolor": INK, "axes.labelcolor": INK, "xtick.color": INK, "ytick.color": INK,
    25	         "axes.linewidth": 0.5, "axes.spines.top": False, "axes.spines.right": False, "axes.grid": False,
    26	         "figure.facecolor": "white", "axes.facecolor": "white", "savefig.facecolor": "white",
    27	         "savefig.dpi": 200, "savefig.bbox": "tight"}
    28	COLOR = {"pin": COBALT, "remember": ROSE, "window": GREY}
    29	
    30	
    31	def main():
    32	    summary = json.loads((HERE / "results" / "summary.json").read_text())
    33	    length = int(summary["key_length"])
    34	    order = summary["model_order"]
    35	    counts = summary["counts"]
    36	    medians = summary["median_first_error"]
    37	    plt.rcParams.update(STYLE)
    38	    fig, (a, b) = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.4, 3.1), gridspec_kw={"wspace": 0.32})
    39	    xs = list(range(len(order)))
    40	    width = 0.24
    41	    for i, policy in enumerate(("pin", "remember", "window")):
    42	        vals = []
    43	        for m in order:
    44	            cell = counts[m][policy][str(length)]
    45	            vals.append(cell["exact"] / cell["n"] if cell["n"] else 0)
    46	        a.bar([x + (i - 1) * width for x in xs], vals, width=width, color=COLOR[policy], label=policy)
    47	    a.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
    48	    a.set_yticks([0, 0.5, 1])
    49	    a.set_ylabel(f"fraction exact at {length}")
    50	    a.set_xticks(xs)
    51	    a.set_xticklabels(order, rotation=90)
    52	    a.legend(fontsize=6, ncol=3, loc="lower left", bbox_to_anchor=(0.22, 1.02), frameon=False, borderaxespad=0)
    53	    a.set_title("a  Exact runs", loc="left", color=INK, fontsize=7)
    54	    for policy, marker, dx in (("remember", "o", -0.12), ("window", "s", 0.12)):
    55	        pts = [(x, medians[policy][m]) for x, m in zip(xs, order) if medians[policy][m] is not None]
    56	        if pts:
    57	            b.plot([p[0] + dx for p in pts], [p[1] for p in pts], marker, color=COLOR[policy], label=policy, ls="none")
    58	    b.set_xticks(xs)
    59	    b.set_xticklabels(order, rotation=90)
    60	    b.set_ylabel("first error step")
    61	    ys = [v for policy in ("remember", "window") for v in medians[policy].values() if v]
    62	    if ys and max(ys) / max(1, min(ys)) >= 8:
    63	        b.set_yscale("log")
    64	    b.legend(fontsize=6)
    65	    b.set_title("b  Lower median first error", loc="left", color=INK, fontsize=7)
    66	    out = HERE / "figs"
    67	    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    68	    fig.savefig(out / "remember.png")
    69	    print(json.dumps({"wrote": str(HERE / "figs" / "remember.png"), "models": len(order), "key_length": length}))

    73	    main()
     1	"""

     3	
     4	White background. Cobalt is pin, rose is remember. Solid is E-pin, dashed is E-hist.
     5	The numbers in the titles are the summary's E-hist remember counts. Matplotlib only.

     8	"""
     9

    11	
    12	import json
    13	import pathlib
    14	import sys
    15	
    16	import matplotlib
    17	
    18	matplotlib.use("Agg")
    19	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402

    21	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent

    23	INK = "#1F2A44"

    25	         "font.size": 7, "axes.labelsize": 7, "xtick.labelsize": 6, "ytick.labelsize": 6,

    27	         "axes.linewidth": 0.5, "axes.spines.top": False, "axes.spines.right": False, "axes.grid": False,
    28	         "figure.facecolor": "white", "axes.facecolor": "white", "savefig.facecolor": "white",
    29	         "savefig.dpi": 200, "savefig.bbox": "tight"}
    30	SERIES = (
    31	    ("e-pin", "pin", "-", "E-pin, pin", COBALT),
    32	    ("e-hist", "pin", "--", "E-hist, pin", COBALT),
    33	    ("e-pin", "remember", "-", "E-pin, remember", ROSE),
    34	    ("e-hist", "remember", "--", "E-hist, remember", ROSE),
    35	)

    37	
    38	def draw(summary, out_path):
    39	    plt.rcParams.update(STYLE)
    40	    order = summary["model_order"]
    41	    lengths = [int(x) for x in summary["lengths"]]
    42	    key = int(summary["key_length"])
    43	    counts = summary["counts"]
    44	    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(7.2, 5.6), sharey=True)
    45	    handles = None
    46	    for ax, name in zip(axes.ravel(), order):
    47	        for edit, policy, ls, label, color in SERIES:
    48	            ys = []
    49	            for L in lengths:
    50	                cell = counts[name][edit][policy][str(L)]
    51	                ys.append(cell["exact"] / cell["n"] if cell["n"] else float("nan"))
    52	            line, = ax.plot(lengths, ys, ls=ls, color=color, marker="o", ms=3.2, lw=1.5, label=label)
    53	            if handles is None:
    54	                handles = []
    55	            if len(handles) < len(SERIES):
    56	                handles.append(line)
    57	        cell = counts[name]["e-hist"]["remember"][str(key)]
    58	        ax.set_title(f"{name}   {cell['exact']}/{cell['n']}", loc="left", color=INK, fontsize=7)
    59	        ax.set_xscale("log")
    60	        ax.set_xticks(lengths)
    61	        ax.set_xticklabels([str(L) for L in lengths])
    62	        ax.xaxis.set_minor_formatter(plt.NullFormatter())
    63	        ax.tick_params(axis="x", which="minor", length=0)
    64	        ax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.05)
    65	        ax.set_yticks([0, 0.5, 1])
    66	        ax.set_xlabel("events")
    67	    axes[0, 0].set_ylabel("fraction exact")
    68	    axes[1, 0].set_ylabel("fraction exact")
    69	    seed = ", ".join(str(s) for s in summary.get("seeds", []))
    70	    fig.suptitle(f"seed {seed}    tapes {summary.get('tapes')}    key {key}", color=INK, fontsize=8, y=0.98)
    71	    fig.legend(handles, [s[3] for s in SERIES], loc="upper center", ncol=4, frameon=False,
    72	               bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.93), fontsize=6)
    73	    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.90))
    74	    out_path = pathlib.Path(out_path)
    75	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    76	    fig.savefig(out_path)
    77	    plt.close(fig)
    78	    return out_path
    79	
    80	
    81	def main(summary_path=None, out_path=None):
    82	    path = pathlib.Path(summary_path) if summary_path else HERE / "results2" / "summary.json"
    83	    summary = json.loads(path.read_text())
    84	    dest = pathlib.Path(out_path) if out_path else HERE / "figs" / "remember2.png"
    85	    draw(summary, dest)
    86	    print(json.dumps({"wrote": str(dest), "models": len(summary["model_order"]),
    87	                      "key_length": summary["key_length"]}))
    88	
    89	
    90	if __name__ == "__main__":
    91	    main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None)

   292	def train(model, ctx, a, kind, lr, out_dir, val_tables, init=None, start=0, steps=None, ckpts=None):
   293	    """One edit trained from the loaded base model with BIGIDEA's recipe. kind 'e7' (vector) or 'e3' (LoRA r1 qkvo).
   294	    `init` (a saved steer.pt dict) and `start` continue an E7 run from step `start` (Amendment 3): the data stream is
   295	    advanced past the first `start` steps' examples and the schedule uses the global step; Adam starts fresh.
   296	    Returns (model, steer or None, record)."""
   297	    import torch
   298	    c = U.core(model)
   299	    torch.manual_seed(a.train_seed)
   300	    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
   301	    model.enable_input_require_grads()
   302	    steer = None
   303	    if kind == "e7":
   304	        for p in model.parameters():
   305	            p.requires_grad_(False)
   306	        steer = U.Steer(model, ctx["steer_layer"] if init is None else init["layer"], "block",
   307	                        None if init is None else init["vec"])
   308	        params = [steer.vec]
   309	        edit = {"kind": "steer", "layer": ctx["steer_layer"], "where": "block"}
   310	    else:
   311	        from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model
   312	        names = {n.split(".")[-1] for n, _ in c.named_modules()}
   313	        targets = ["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"] if "q_proj" in names else ["qkv_proj", "o_proj"]
   314	        cfg = LoraConfig(r=1, lora_alpha=2, lora_dropout=0.0, bias="none", task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
   315	                         target_modules=targets, layers_to_transform=None)
   316	        model = get_peft_model(model, cfg)
   317	        c = U.core(model)
   318	        params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
   319	        edit = {"kind": "lora", "rank": 1, "targets": targets, "layers": None}
   320	    n_params = int(sum(p.numel() for p in params))
   321	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=lr, betas=(0.9, 0.99), weight_decay=0.0)
   322	    pin_rng = random.Random(a.train_seed + 7919)
   323	    pool, prefix = ctx["pool"], ctx["prefix"]
   324	    for _ in range(start * a.tables):
   325	        pb_pin.pinned_example(pin_rng, pool, "train", a.blocks)
   326	    pin_n = a.tables * a.blocks
   327	    steps = steps or a.steps
   328	    ckpts = sorted({s for s in (ckpts or a.ckpts) if start < s <= steps} | {steps})
   329	    rec = {"kind": kind, "edit": edit, "lr": lr, "steps": steps, "warmup": a.warmup, "trainable_params": n_params,
   330	           "log": [], "val": {}, "saved": {}, "start": start}
   331	    if a.val0:
   332	        model.eval()
   333	        rec["val"]["0"] = fast_eval(model, ctx, val_tables, steer)
   334	        log(kind=kind, step=0, val=rec["val"]["0"])
   335	    t0 = time.time()
   336	    log(train=kind, lr=lr, trainable_params=n_params)
   337	    for step in range(start, steps):
   338	        cur = lr * min(1.0, (step + 1) / max(1, a.warmup))
   339	        for g in opt.param_groups:
   340	            g["lr"] = cur
   341	        model.train()
   342	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
   343	        exs = [pb_pin.pinned_example(pin_rng, pool, "train", a.blocks) for _ in range(a.tables)]
   344	        for ex in exs:
   345	            ex["pi"] = list(prefix) + ex["pi"]
   346	        exs.sort(key=lambda ex: len(ex["pi"]))
   347	        loss_sum, ok = 0.0, 0
   348	        for k in range(0, len(exs), a.micro):
   349	            ids, pos, allow, r, cidx, lab = pb_pin.packed_batch(exs[k:k + a.micro], 0)
   350	            if steer is not None:
   351	                P = torch.tensor([len(ex["pi"]) for ex in exs[k:k + a.micro]])[:, None]
   352	                steer.mask = (torch.arange(ids.shape[1])[None] >= P).cuda()
   353	            h = c.model(input_ids=ids.cuda(), position_ids=pos.cuda(),
   354	                        attention_mask=U.model_mask(c, allow.cuda())).last_hidden_state
   355	            logits = U.head_logits(c, h[r.cuda(), cidx.cuda()])
   356	            tgt = lab.cuda()
   357	            loss = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(logits, tgt, reduction="sum")
   358	            (loss / pin_n).backward()
   359	            if steer is not None:
   360	                steer.mask = None
   361	            loss_sum += float(loss.detach())
   362	            ok += int((logits.argmax(-1) == tgt).sum())
   363	            del h, loss, logits
   364	        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
   365	        opt.step()
   366	        entry = {"step": step + 1, "loss": round(loss_sum / pin_n, 5), "acc": round(ok / pin_n, 5), "lr": cur,
   367	                 "grad_norm": round(float(gn), 4), "elapsed": round(time.time() - t0, 1)}
   368	        rec["log"].append(entry)
   369	        if step % 10 == 0 or step + 1 in ckpts:
   370	            log(**entry)
   371	        if step + 1 in ckpts:
   372	            model.eval()
   373	            d = out_dir / f"step{step + 1}"
   374	            if kind == "e7":
   375	                steer.save(d)
   376	            else:
   377	                model.save_pretrained(d)
   378	            rec["saved"][str(step + 1)] = str(d)
   379	            rec["val"][str(step + 1)] = fast_eval(model, ctx, val_tables, steer)
   380	            log(kind=kind, step=step + 1, val=rec["val"][str(step + 1)])
   381	    base = c if kind == "e3" else model
   382	    base.gradient_checkpointing_disable()
   383	    base.disable_input_require_grads()
   384	    model.eval()
   385	    accs = [e["acc"] for e in rec["log"]]
   386	    losses = [e["loss"] for e in rec["log"]]
   387	    tail = sum(accs[-10:]) / len(accs[-10:])
   388	    mid = sum(accs[20:30]) / max(1, len(accs[20:30])) if len(accs) >= 30 else None
   389	    rec["diverged"] = bool(any(not math.isfinite(x) for x in losses) or (mid is not None and tail < mid - 0.02))
   390	    rec["acc_steps_21_30"], rec["acc_last10"] = mid, tail
   391	    rec["wall"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   392	    return model, steer, rec

   185	    for p in full:

   190	    shortest = min(lengths) if lengths else length

   241	def threats(s):
   242	    L = s["key_length"]
   243	    k = s["keys"]
   244	    t1 = (s["cert_failed_main"] == 0 and k["pin_bitwise"] == k["pin_ticks"] and k["pin_ticks"] > 0
   245	          and s["pin_off_table"] == 0 and s["pin_mismatch"] == 0
   246	          and s["pin_trace_exact_runs"] == s["pin_runs"] and s["pin_runs"] > 0)
   247	    d1 = (f"Main tables that failed re-certification: {s['cert_failed_main']}. "
   248	          f"Pin trace-exact runs at {L}: {s['pin_trace_exact_runs']} of {s['pin_runs']}. "
   249	          f"Pin ticks bitwise equal to the certified row: {k['pin_bitwise']} of {k['pin_ticks']}. "
   250	          f"Off-table pin ticks: {s['pin_off_table']}. Pin logit mismatches: {s['pin_mismatch']}.")
   251	    t2 = s["remember_stream_n"] > 0 and s["remember_stream_ok"] == s["remember_stream_n"]
   252	    d2 = (f"Main remember runs: {s['remember_stream_n']}. "
   253	          f"Runs with stream_tokens = 2 × ticks and max_delta = 0: {s['remember_stream_ok']}.")
   254	    t3 = s["full_attention_both"] >= 1 and s["cert_failed_main"] == 0
   255	    sliding = ", ".join(f"{m} {int((s['layers'].get(m) or {}).get('n_sliding_layers') or 0)}"
   256	                        for m in s["model_order"])
   257	    d3 = (f"Full-attention models (n_sliding_layers = 0): {s['full_attention_models']}. "
   258	          f"Of those, pin trace-exact and remember with no trace-exact run at {L}: {s['full_attention_both']}. "
   259	          f"n_sliding_layers by model: {sliding}.")
   260	    return [
   261	        {"id": 1, "name": "Pin is not the certified function.", "ruled_out": t1, "detail": d1},
   262	        {"id": 2, "name": "Remember evicts or restarts positions.", "ruled_out": t2, "detail": d2},
   263	        {"id": 3, "name": "The result is one architecture's sliding window, or uncertified tables.",
   264	         "ruled_out": t3, "detail": d3},
   265	    ]

   275	    n = k["main_models"]
   276	    z = k["remember_models_zero_exact"]
   277	    lines = [
   278	        f"At {L} events, remember is trace-exact on no run in {z} of {n} models.",
   279	        f"Lower median first-error step on inexact main remember runs at {L}: "
   280	        f"{_fmt_median(k['remember_median_first_error'])} "
    44	_BI_CACHE = bi_stream.cache_from
    45	_ROT = {"max_delta": 0}

    47	
    48	def _cos_sin(rot, delta, like):
    49	    import torch
    50	    pos = torch.tensor([[int(delta)]], device=like.device)
    51	    try:
    52	        return rot(torch.zeros(1, dtype=torch.float32, device=like.device), pos)
    53	    except Exception:
    54	        return rot(like, pos)
    55	
    56	
    57	def rotate_impl(model, kvs, delta):
    58	    """R(delta) on cached post-RoPE keys. Matches bi_stream.rotate_kv on a full-head RoPE,
    59	    and rotates only the rotary subspace when cos is shorter than the head (partial RoPE)."""
    60	    import torch
    61	    if delta == 0 or not kvs:
    62	        return kvs
    63	    c = U.core(model)
    64	    mod = importlib.import_module(type(c).__module__)
    65	    rotate_half = mod.rotate_half
    66	    rot = c.model.rotary_emb
    67	    cos, sin = _cos_sin(rot, delta, kvs[0][0])
    68	    cos, sin = cos.float(), sin.float()
    69	    if cos.ndim == 2:  # (seq, dim) -> (1, seq, dim)
    70	        cos, sin = cos[None], sin[None]
    71	    if cos.ndim == 3:  # (batch, seq, dim) -> (batch, 1, seq, dim), as bi_stream
    72	        cos, sin = cos[:, None], sin[:, None]
    73	    scale = getattr(rot, "attention_scaling", 1.0)
    74	    try:
    75	        scale = float(scale)
    76	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    77	        scale = 1.0
    78	    # A scaled rotary embedding multiplies cos and sin by attention_scaling. The cached key
    79	    # was already scaled once; the increment has to be a pure rotation or the scale is applied twice.
    80	    if scale != 1.0:
    81	        cos, sin = cos / scale, sin / scale
    82	    rotary_dim = cos.shape[-1]
    83	    out = []
    84	    for k, v in kvs:
    85	        kf = k.float()
    86	        if rotary_dim == kf.shape[-1]:
    87	            kr = kf * cos + rotate_half(kf) * sin
    88	        elif rotary_dim < kf.shape[-1]:
    89	            head, tail = kf[..., :rotary_dim], kf[..., rotary_dim:]
    90	            kr = torch.cat((head * cos + rotate_half(head) * sin, tail), -1)
    91	        else:
    92	            raise RuntimeError(f"rotary dim {rotary_dim} exceeds head {kf.shape[-1]}")
    93	        out.append((kr.to(dtype=k.dtype), v))
    94	    return out
    95	
    96	
    97	def rotate_kv(model, kvs, delta):
    98	    _ROT["max_delta"] = max(_ROT["max_delta"], int(delta))
    99	    # Qwen3 uses bi_stream's rotation unchanged. Other models use the same math with their rotate_half.
   100	    if type(U.core(model)).__name__.startswith("Qwen3"):
   101	        return _BI_ROTATE(model, kvs, delta)
   102	    return rotate_impl(model, kvs, delta)
   103	
   104	
   105	def cache_from(template, kvs):
   106	    """bi_stream's cache, then: a sliding layer is given only the last sliding_window-1 of the keys
   107	    the policy selected, and its cumulative_length is that count. That is the cache the layer keeps
   108	    in its own forward. Full-attention layers receive every selected key."""
   109	    new = _BI_CACHE(template, kvs)
   110	    for layer in new.layers:
   111	        if not getattr(layer, "is_sliding", False) or getattr(layer, "keys", None) is None:
   112	            continue
   113	        w = int(layer.sliding_window)
   114	        if layer.keys.shape[-2] > w - 1:
   115	            layer.keys = layer.keys[..., -(w - 1):, :].contiguous()
   116	            layer.values = layer.values[..., -(w - 1):, :].contiguous()
   117	        layer.cumulative_length = int(layer.keys.shape[-2])
   118	        if hasattr(layer, "is_initialized"):
   119	            layer.is_initialized = True
   120	    return new
   121	
   122	
   123	def install_patches():
   124	    bi_stream.rotate_kv = rotate_kv
   125	    bi_stream.cache_from = cache_from
